		public BrowserSettings(bool autoDispose = false)
		{
			settings = new CefSharp.Core.BrowserSettings(autoDispose);
		}

		public void Dispose()
		{
			settings.Dispose();
		}

		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		public IBrowserSettings UnWrap()
		{
			return settings;
		}

		public static IBrowserSettings Create(bool autoDispose = false)
		{
			return new CefSharp.Core.BrowserSettings(autoDispose);
		}

	public static class Cef
	{
		public static TaskFactory UIThreadTaskFactory => CefSharp.Core.Cef.UIThreadTaskFactory;

		public static TaskFactory IOThreadTaskFactory => CefSharp.Core.Cef.IOThreadTaskFactory;

		public static TaskFactory FileThreadTaskFactory => CefSharp.Core.Cef.FileThreadTaskFactory;

		public static bool IsInitialized => CefSharp.Core.Cef.IsInitialized;

		public static bool IsShutdown => CefSharp.Core.Cef.IsShutdown;

		public static string CefSharpVersion => CefSharp.Core.Cef.CefSharpVersion;

		public static string CefVersion => CefSharp.Core.Cef.CefVersion;

		public static string ChromiumVersion => CefSharp.Core.Cef.ChromiumVersion;

		public static string CefCommitHash => CefSharp.Core.Cef.CefCommitHash;

		public static bool CrashReportingEnabled => CefSharp.Core.Cef.CrashReportingEnabled;

		public static event EventHandler ShutdownStarted;

		public static void AddDisposable(IDisposable item)
		{
			CefSharp.Core.Cef.AddDisposable(item);
		}

		public static void RemoveDisposable(IDisposable item)
		{
			CefSharp.Core.Cef.RemoveDisposable(item);
		}

		public static UrlParts ParseUrl(string url)
		{
			return CefSharp.Core.Cef.ParseUrl(url);
		}

		public static bool Initialize(CefSettingsBase settings)
		{
			using (settings)
			{
				return CefSharp.Core.Cef.Initialize(settings.settings);
			}
		}

		public static bool Initialize(CefSettingsBase settings, bool performDependencyCheck)
		{
			using (settings)
			{
				return CefSharp.Core.Cef.Initialize(settings.settings, performDependencyCheck);
			}
		}

		public static bool Initialize(CefSettingsBase settings, bool performDependencyCheck, IBrowserProcessHandler browserProcessHandler)
		{
			using (settings)
			{
				return CefSharp.Core.Cef.Initialize(settings.settings, performDependencyCheck, browserProcessHandler);
			}
		}

		public static bool Initialize(CefSettingsBase settings, bool performDependencyCheck, IApp cefApp)
		{
			using (settings)
			{
				return CefSharp.Core.Cef.Initialize(settings.settings, performDependencyCheck, cefApp);
			}
		}

		public static Task<bool> InitializeAsync(CefSettingsBase settings, bool performDependencyCheck = true, IBrowserProcessHandler browserProcessHandler = null)
		{
			using (settings)
			{
				try
				{
					CefSharp.Core.Cef.Initialize(settings.settings, performDependencyCheck, browserProcessHandler);
				}
				catch (Exception exception)
				{
					GlobalContextInitialized.SetException(exception);
				}
			}
			return GlobalContextInitialized.Task;
		}

		public static void RunMessageLoop()
		{
			CefSharp.Core.Cef.RunMessageLoop();
		}

		public static void QuitMessageLoop()
		{
			CefSharp.Core.Cef.QuitMessageLoop();
		}

		public static void DoMessageLoopWork()
		{
			CefSharp.Core.Cef.DoMessageLoopWork();
		}

		public static int ExecuteProcess()
		{
			return CefSharp.Core.Cef.ExecuteProcess();
		}

		public static bool AddCrossOriginWhitelistEntry(string sourceOrigin, string targetProtocol, string targetDomain, bool allowTargetSubdomains)
		{
			return CefSharp.Core.Cef.AddCrossOriginWhitelistEntry(sourceOrigin, targetProtocol, targetDomain, allowTargetSubdomains);
		}

		public static bool RemoveCrossOriginWhitelistEntry(string sourceOrigin, string targetProtocol, string targetDomain, bool allowTargetSubdomains)
		{
			return CefSharp.Core.Cef.RemoveCrossOriginWhitelistEntry(sourceOrigin, targetProtocol, targetDomain, allowTargetSubdomains);
		}

		public static bool ClearCrossOriginWhitelist()
		{
			return CefSharp.Core.Cef.ClearCrossOriginWhitelist();
		}

		public static ICookieManager GetGlobalCookieManager(ICompletionCallback callback = null)
		{
			return CefSharp.Core.Cef.GetGlobalCookieManager(callback);
		}

		public static void PreShutdown()
		{
			CefSharp.Core.Cef.PreShutdown();
		}

		public static void Shutdown()
		{
			Cef.ShutdownStarted?.Invoke(null, EventArgs.Empty);
			Cef.ShutdownStarted = null;
			CefSharp.Core.Cef.Shutdown();
		}

		public static void ShutdownWithoutChecks()
		{
			Cef.ShutdownStarted?.Invoke(null, EventArgs.Empty);
			Cef.ShutdownStarted = null;
			CefSharp.Core.Cef.ShutdownWithoutChecks();
		}

		public static bool ClearSchemeHandlerFactories()
		{
			return CefSharp.Core.Cef.ClearSchemeHandlerFactories();
		}

		public static bool CurrentlyOnThread(CefThreadIds threadId)
		{
			return CefSharp.Core.Cef.CurrentlyOnThread(threadId);
		}

		public static IRequestContext GetGlobalRequestContext()
		{
			return CefSharp.Core.Cef.GetGlobalRequestContext();
		}

		public static uint ColorSetARGB(uint a, uint r, uint g, uint b)
		{
			return CefSharp.Core.Cef.ColorSetARGB(a, r, g, b);
		}

		public static void SetCrashKeyValue(string key, string value)
		{
			CefSharp.Core.Cef.SetCrashKeyValue(key, value);
		}

		public static LogSeverity GetMinLogLevel()
		{
			int minLogLevel = CefSharp.Core.Cef.GetMinLogLevel();
			return minLogLevel switch
			{
				-1 => LogSeverity.Verbose, 
				0 => LogSeverity.Info, 
				1 => LogSeverity.Warning, 
				2 => LogSeverity.Error, 
				3 => LogSeverity.Fatal, 
				_ => (LogSeverity)minLogLevel, 
			};
		}

		public static string GetMimeType(string extension)
		{
			return CefSharp.Core.Cef.GetMimeType(extension);
		}

		public static void EnableWaitForBrowsersToClose()
		{
			CefSharp.Core.Cef.EnableWaitForBrowsersToClose();
		}

		public static void WaitForBrowsersToClose()
		{
			CefSharp.Core.Cef.WaitForBrowsersToClose();
		}

		public static void WaitForBrowsersToClose(int timeoutInMiliseconds)
		{
			CefSharp.Core.Cef.WaitForBrowsersToClose(timeoutInMiliseconds);
		}

		public static bool PostDelayedAction(CefThreadIds threadId, Action action, int delayInMs)
		{
			return CefSharp.Core.Cef.PostDelayedAction(threadId, action, delayInMs);
		}

		public static bool PostAction(CefThreadIds threadId, Action action)
		{
			return CefSharp.Core.Cef.PostAction(threadId, action);
		}
	}
	public abstract class CefSettingsBase : IDisposable
	{
		private bool disposed;

		internal CefSharp.Core.CefSettingsBase settings = new CefSharp.Core.CefSettingsBase();

		public bool IsDisposed => disposed;

		public IEnumerable<CefCustomScheme> CefCustomSchemes => settings.CefCustomSchemes;

		public CommandLineArgDictionary CefCommandLineArgs => settings.CefCommandLineArgs;

		public bool ChromeRuntime
		{
			get
			{
				return settings.ChromeRuntime;
			}
			set
			{
				settings.ChromeRuntime = value;
			}
		}

		public bool CommandLineArgsDisabled
		{
			get
			{
				return settings.CommandLineArgsDisabled;
			}
			set
			{
				settings.CommandLineArgsDisabled = value;
			}
		}

		public bool ExternalMessagePump
		{
			get
			{
				return settings.ExternalMessagePump;
			}
			set
			{
				settings.ExternalMessagePump = value;
			}
		}

		public bool MultiThreadedMessageLoop
		{
			get
			{
				return settings.MultiThreadedMessageLoop;
			}
			set
			{
				settings.MultiThreadedMessageLoop = value;
			}
		}

		public string BrowserSubprocessPath
		{
			get
			{
				return settings.BrowserSubprocessPath;
			}
			set
			{
				settings.BrowserSubprocessPath = value;
			}
		}

		public string CachePath
		{
			get
			{
				return settings.CachePath;
			}
			set
			{
				settings.CachePath = value;
			}
		}

		public string RootCachePath
		{
			get
			{
				return settings.RootCachePath;
			}
			set
			{
				settings.RootCachePath = value;
			}
		}

		public string UserDataPath
		{
			get
			{
				return settings.UserDataPath;
			}
			set
			{
				settings.UserDataPath = value;
			}
		}

		public bool IgnoreCertificateErrors
		{
			get
			{
				return settings.CefCommandLineArgs.ContainsKey("ignore-certificate-errors");
			}
			set
			{
				if (value)
				{
					if (!settings.CefCommandLineArgs.ContainsKey("ignore-certificate-errors"))
					{
						settings.CefCommandLineArgs.Add("ignore-certificate-errors");
					}
				}
				else if (settings.CefCommandLineArgs.ContainsKey("ignore-certificate-errors"))
				{
					settings.CefCommandLineArgs.Remove("ignore-certificate-errors");
				}
			}
		}

		public string Locale
		{
			get
			{
				return settings.Locale;
			}
			set
			{
				settings.Locale = value;
			}
		}

		public string LocalesDirPath
		{
			get
			{
				return settings.LocalesDirPath;
			}
			set
			{
				settings.LocalesDirPath = value;
			}
		}

		public string ResourcesDirPath
		{
			get
			{
				return settings.ResourcesDirPath;
			}
			set
			{
				settings.ResourcesDirPath = value;
			}
		}

		public string LogFile
		{
			get
			{
				return settings.LogFile;
			}
			set
			{
				settings.LogFile = value;
			}
		}

		public LogSeverity LogSeverity
		{
			get
			{
				return settings.LogSeverity;
			}
			set
			{
				settings.LogSeverity = value;
			}
		}

		public string JavascriptFlags
		{
			get
			{
				return settings.JavascriptFlags;
			}
			set
			{
				settings.JavascriptFlags = value;
			}
		}

		public bool PackLoadingDisabled
		{
			get
			{
				return settings.PackLoadingDisabled;
			}
			set
			{
				settings.PackLoadingDisabled = value;
			}
		}

		public string UserAgentProduct
		{
			get
			{
				return settings.UserAgentProduct;
			}
			set
			{
				settings.UserAgentProduct = value;
			}
		}

		public int RemoteDebuggingPort
		{
			get
			{
				return settings.RemoteDebuggingPort;
			}
			set
			{
				settings.RemoteDebuggingPort = value;
			}
		}

		public int UncaughtExceptionStackSize
		{
			get
			{
				return settings.UncaughtExceptionStackSize;
			}
			set
			{
				settings.UncaughtExceptionStackSize = value;
			}
		}

		public string UserAgent
		{
			get
			{
				return settings.UserAgent;
			}
			set
			{
				settings.UserAgent = value;
			}
		}

		public bool WindowlessRenderingEnabled
		{
			get
			{
				return settings.WindowlessRenderingEnabled;
			}
			set
			{
				settings.WindowlessRenderingEnabled = value;
			}
		}

		public bool PersistSessionCookies
		{
			get
			{
				return settings.PersistSessionCookies;
			}
			set
			{
				settings.PersistSessionCookies = value;
			}
		}

		public bool PersistUserPreferences
		{
			get
			{
				return settings.PersistUserPreferences;
			}
			set
			{
				settings.PersistUserPreferences = value;
			}
		}

		public string AcceptLanguageList
		{
			get
			{
				return settings.AcceptLanguageList;
			}
			set
			{
				settings.AcceptLanguageList = value;
			}
		}

		public uint BackgroundColor
		{
			get
			{
				return settings.BackgroundColor;
			}
			set
			{
				settings.BackgroundColor = value;
			}
		}

		public string CookieableSchemesList
		{
			get
			{
				return settings.CookieableSchemesList;
			}
			set
			{
				settings.CookieableSchemesList = value;
			}
		}

		public bool CookieableSchemesExcludeDefaults
		{
			get
			{
				return settings.CookieableSchemesExcludeDefaults;
			}
			set
			{
				settings.CookieableSchemesExcludeDefaults = value;
			}
		}

		public void Dispose()
		{
			disposed = true;
			settings = null;
		}

		public void RegisterScheme(CefCustomScheme scheme)
		{
			settings.RegisterScheme(scheme);
		}

		public void DisableGpuAcceleration()
		{
			if (!settings.CefCommandLineArgs.ContainsKey("disable-gpu"))
			{
				settings.CefCommandLineArgs.Add("disable-gpu");
			}
		}

		public void EnablePrintPreview()
		{
			if (!settings.CefCommandLineArgs.ContainsKey("enable-print-preview"))
			{
				settings.CefCommandLineArgs.Add("enable-print-preview");
			}
		}

		public void SetOffScreenRenderingBestPerformanceArgs()
		{
			if (!settings.CefCommandLineArgs.ContainsKey("disable-gpu"))
			{
				settings.CefCommandLineArgs.Add("disable-gpu");
			}
			if (!settings.CefCommandLineArgs.ContainsKey("disable-gpu-compositing"))
			{
				settings.CefCommandLineArgs.Add("disable-gpu-compositing");
			}
			if (!settings.CefCommandLineArgs.ContainsKey("enable-begin-frame-scheduling"))
			{
				settings.CefCommandLineArgs.Add("enable-begin-frame-scheduling");
			}
		}
	}
	public static class DevToolsExtensions
	{
		public static int ExecuteDevToolsMethod(this IBrowserHost browserHost, int messageId, string method, JsonString parameters)
		{
			WebBrowserExtensions.ThrowExceptionIfBrowserHostNull(browserHost);
			string paramsAsJson = parameters?.Json;
			return browserHost.ExecuteDevToolsMethod(messageId, method, paramsAsJson);
		}

		public static Task<int> ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync(this IBrowser browser, int messageId, string method, IDictionary<string, object> parameters = null)
		{
			WebBrowserExtensions.ThrowExceptionIfBrowserNull(browser);
			IBrowserHost browserHost = browser.GetHost();
			WebBrowserExtensions.ThrowExceptionIfBrowserHostNull(browserHost);
			if (CefThread.CurrentlyOnUiThread)
			{
				return Task.FromResult(browserHost.ExecuteDevToolsMethod(messageId, method, parameters));
			}
			if (CefThread.CanExecuteOnUiThread)
			{
				return CefThread.ExecuteOnUiThread(() => browserHost.ExecuteDevToolsMethod(messageId, method, parameters));
			}
			return Task.FromResult(0);
		}

		public static Task<int> ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync(this IChromiumWebBrowserBase chromiumWebBrowser, int messageId, string method, IDictionary<string, object> parameters = null)
		{
			IBrowser browserCore = chromiumWebBrowser.BrowserCore;
			return browserCore.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync(messageId, method, parameters);
		}

		public static DevToolsClient GetDevToolsClient(this IChromiumWebBrowserBase chromiumWebBrowser)
		{
			IBrowser browserCore = chromiumWebBrowser.BrowserCore;
			return browserCore.GetDevToolsClient();
		}

		public static DevToolsClient GetDevToolsClient(this IBrowser browser)
		{
			WebBrowserExtensions.ThrowExceptionIfBrowserNull(browser);
			IBrowserHost host = browser.GetHost();
			WebBrowserExtensions.ThrowExceptionIfBrowserHostNull(host);
			DevToolsClient devToolsClient = new DevToolsClient(browser);
			IRegistration devToolsObserverRegistration = host.AddDevToolsMessageObserver(devToolsClient);
			devToolsClient.SetDevToolsObserverRegistration(devToolsObserverRegistration);
			return devToolsClient;
		}

		public static Task<bool> SetMainFrameDocumentContentAsync(this IChromiumWebBrowserBase chromiumWebBrowser, string html)
		{
			IBrowser browserCore = chromiumWebBrowser.BrowserCore;
			return browserCore.SetMainFrameDocumentContentAsync(html);
		}

		public static async Task<bool> SetMainFrameDocumentContentAsync(this IBrowser browser, string html)
		{
			WebBrowserExtensions.ThrowExceptionIfBrowserNull(browser);
			using DevToolsClient client = browser.GetDevToolsClient();
			FrameTree frameTree = (await client.Page.GetFrameTreeAsync().ConfigureAwait(continueOnCapturedContext: false)).FrameTree;
			Frame frame = frameTree.Frame;
			return (await client.Page.SetDocumentContentAsync(frame.Id, html).ConfigureAwait(continueOnCapturedContext: false)).Success;
		}
	}

	public class RequestContext : IRequestContext, IDisposable
	{
		private CefSharp.Core.RequestContext requestContext;

		public bool IsGlobal => requestContext.IsGlobal;

		public string CachePath => requestContext.CachePath;

		public bool IsDisposed => requestContext.IsDisposed;

		public RequestContext()
		{
			requestContext = new CefSharp.Core.RequestContext();
		}

		public RequestContext(IRequestContext otherRequestContext)
		{
			if (otherRequestContext == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("otherRequestContext");
			}
			requestContext = new CefSharp.Core.RequestContext(otherRequestContext);
		}

		public RequestContext(IRequestContext otherRequestContext, IRequestContextHandler requestContextHandler)
		{
			if (otherRequestContext == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("otherRequestContext");
			}
			if (requestContextHandler == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("requestContextHandler");
			}
			requestContext = new CefSharp.Core.RequestContext(otherRequestContext, requestContextHandler);
		}

		public RequestContext(IRequestContextHandler requestContextHandler)
		{
			if (requestContextHandler == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("requestContextHandler");
			}
			requestContext = new CefSharp.Core.RequestContext(requestContextHandler);
		}

		public RequestContext(RequestContextSettings settings)
		{
			if (settings == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("settings");
			}
			requestContext = new CefSharp.Core.RequestContext(settings.settings);
		}

		public RequestContext(RequestContextSettings settings, IRequestContextHandler requestContextHandler)
		{

			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("settings");
			}
			if (requestContextHandler == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("requestContextHandler");

			requestContext = new CefSharp.Core.RequestContext(settings.settings, requestContextHandler);
		}

		public void LoadExtension(string rootDirectory, string manifestJson, IExtensionHandler handler)
		{
			if (Cef.CurrentlyOnThread(CefThreadIds.TID_UI))
			{
				requestContext.LoadExtension(rootDirectory, manifestJson, handler);
				return;
			}
			Cef.UIThreadTaskFactory.StartNew(delegate
			{
				requestContext.LoadExtension(rootDirectory, manifestJson, handler);
			});
		}

		public void Dispose()

		}

		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		public IRequestContext UnWrap()
		{
			return requestContext;
		}
	}
	public class RequestContextBuilder
	{
		private RequestContextSettings _settings;

		private IRequestContext _otherContext;

		private RequestContextHandler _handler;

		private void ThrowExceptionIfContextAlreadySet()
		{
			if (_otherContext != null)
			{
				throw new Exception("A call to WithSharedSettings has already been made, it is no possible to provide custom settings.");
			}
		}

		private void ThrowExceptionIfCustomSettingSpecified()
		{
			if (_settings != null)
			{
				throw new Exception("A call to WithCachePath/PersistUserPreferences has already been made, it's not possible to share settings with another RequestContext.");
			}
		}

		public IRequestContext Create()
		{
			if (_otherContext != null)
			{
				return new CefSharp.Core.RequestContext(_otherContext, _handler);
			}
			if (_settings != null)
			{
				return new CefSharp.Core.RequestContext(_settings.settings, _handler);
			}
			if (_handler != null)
			{
				return new CefSharp.Core.RequestContext(_handler);
			}
			return new CefSharp.Core.RequestContext();
		}

		public RequestContextBuilder OnInitialize(Action<IRequestContext> action)
		{
			if (_handler == null)
			{
				_handler = new RequestContextHandler();
			}
			_handler.OnInitialize(action);
			return this;
		}

		public RequestContextBuilder WithCachePath(string cachePath)
		{
			ThrowExceptionIfContextAlreadySet();
			if (_settings == null)
			{
				_settings = new RequestContextSettings();
			}
			_settings.CachePath = cachePath;
			return this;
		}

		public RequestContextBuilder PersistUserPreferences()
		{
			ThrowExceptionIfContextAlreadySet();
			if (_settings == null)
			{
				_settings = new RequestContextSettings();
			}
			_settings.PersistUserPreferences = true;
			return this;
		}

		public RequestContextBuilder WithPreference(string name, object value)
		{
			if (_handler == null)
			{
				_handler = new RequestContextHandler();
			}
			_handler.SetPreferenceOnContextInitialized(name, value);
			return this;
		}

		public RequestContextBuilder WithProxyServer(string host)
		{
			if (_handler == null)
			{
				_handler = new RequestContextHandler();
			}
			_handler.SetProxyOnContextInitialized(host);
			return this;
		}

		public RequestContextBuilder WithProxyServer(string host, int? port)
		{
			if (_handler == null)
			{
				_handler = new RequestContextHandler();
			}
			_handler.SetProxyOnContextInitialized(host, port);
			return this;
		}

		public RequestContextBuilder WithProxyServer(string scheme, string host, int? port)
		{
			if (_handler == null)
			{
				_handler = new RequestContextHandler();
			}
			_handler.SetProxyOnContextInitialized(scheme, host, port);
			return this;
		}

		public RequestContextBuilder WithSharedSettings(IRequestContext other)
		{
			if (other == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("other");
			}
			ThrowExceptionIfCustomSettingSpecified();
			_otherContext = other;
			return this;
		}
	}

	public class UrlRequest : IUrlRequest, IDisposable
	{
		private CefSharp.Core.UrlRequest urlRequest;

		public bool ResponseWasCached => urlRequest.ResponseWasCached;

		public IResponse Response => urlRequest.Response;

		public UrlRequestStatus RequestStatus => urlRequest.RequestStatus;

		public UrlRequest(IRequest request, IUrlRequestClient urlRequestClient)
			: this(request, urlRequestClient, null)
		{
		}

		public UrlRequest(IRequest request, IUrlRequestClient urlRequestClient, IRequestContext requestContext)
		{
			if (request == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("request");
			}
			if (urlRequestClient == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("urlRequestClient");
			}
			urlRequest = new CefSharp.Core.UrlRequest(request.UnWrap(), urlRequestClient, requestContext?.UnWrap());
		}

		public void Dispose()
		{
			urlRequest.Dispose();
		}

		public IUrlRequest Create(IRequest request, IUrlRequestClient urlRequestClient)
		{
			return new CefSharp.Core.UrlRequest(request, urlRequestClient);
		}

		public IUrlRequest Create(IRequest request, IUrlRequestClient urlRequestClient, IRequestContext requestContext)
		{
			return new CefSharp.Core.UrlRequest(request, urlRequestClient, requestContext);
		}
	}
	public static class WebBrowserExtensionsEx
	{
		public static Task<NavigationEntry> GetVisibleNavigationEntryAsync(this IChromiumWebBrowserBase browser)
		{
			IBrowserHost host = browser.GetBrowserHost();
			if (host == null)
			{
				return Task.FromResult<NavigationEntry>(null);
			}
			if (Cef.CurrentlyOnThread(CefThreadIds.TID_UI))
			{
				NavigationEntry visibleNavigationEntry = host.GetVisibleNavigationEntry();
				return Task.FromResult(visibleNavigationEntry);
			}
			TaskCompletionSource<NavigationEntry> tcs = new TaskCompletionSource<NavigationEntry>();
			Cef.UIThreadTaskFactory.StartNew(delegate
			{
				NavigationEntry visibleNavigationEntry2 = host.GetVisibleNavigationEntry();
				tcs.TrySetResultAsync(visibleNavigationEntry2);
			});
			return tcs.Task;
		}

		public static void DownloadUrl(this IFrame frame, string url, Action<IUrlRequest, Stream> completeHandler)
		{
			if (!frame.IsValid)
			{
				throw new Exception("Frame is invalid, unable to continue.");
			}
			Cef.UIThreadTaskFactory.StartNew(delegate
			{
				IRequest request = frame.CreateRequest(initializePostData: false);
				request.Method = "GET";
				request.Url = url;
				MemoryStream memoryStream = new MemoryStream();
				IUrlRequestClient client = CefSharp.Fluent.UrlRequestClient.Create().OnDownloadData(delegate(IUrlRequest req, Stream stream)
				{
					stream.CopyTo(memoryStream);
				}).OnRequestComplete(delegate(IUrlRequest req)
				{
					memoryStream.Position = 0L;
					completeHandler?.Invoke(req, memoryStream);
				})
					.Build();
				IUrlRequest urlRequest = frame.CreateUrlRequest(request, client);
			});
		}

		public static Task<byte[]> DownloadUrlAsync(this IFrame frame, string url)
		{
			if (!frame.IsValid)
			{
				throw new Exception("Frame is invalid, unable to continue.");
			}
			TaskCompletionSource<byte[]> taskCompletionSource = new TaskCompletionSource<byte[]>();
			Cef.UIThreadTaskFactory.StartNew(delegate
			{
				IRequest request = frame.CreateRequest(initializePostData: false);
				request.Method = "GET";
				request.Url = url;
				MemoryStream memoryStream = new MemoryStream();
				IUrlRequestClient client = CefSharp.Fluent.UrlRequestClient.Create().OnDownloadData(delegate(IUrlRequest req, Stream stream)
				{
					stream.CopyTo(memoryStream);
				}).OnRequestComplete(delegate(IUrlRequest req)
				{
					if (req.RequestStatus == UrlRequestStatus.Success)
					{
						taskCompletionSource.TrySetResultAsync(memoryStream.ToArray());
					}
					else
					{
						taskCompletionSource.TrySetExceptionAsync(new Exception("RequestStatus:" + req.RequestStatus.ToString() + ";StatusCode:" + req.Response.StatusCode));
					}
				})
					.Build();
				IUrlRequest urlRequest = frame.CreateUrlRequest(request, client);
			});
			return taskCompletionSource.Task;
		}

		public static void ToggleAudioMute(this IChromiumWebBrowserBase browser)
		{
			if (browser.IsDisposed || Cef.IsShutdown)
			{
				return;
			}
			Cef.UIThreadTaskFactory.StartNew(delegate
			{
				IBrowser browserCore = browser.BrowserCore;
				if (browserCore != null && !browserCore.IsDisposed)
				{
					IBrowserHost host = browserCore.GetHost();
					bool isAudioMuted = host.IsAudioMuted;
					host.SetAudioMuted(!isAudioMuted);
				}
			});
		}

		public static async Task<T> EvaluateScriptAsync<T>(this IFrame frame, string script, TimeSpan? timeout = null)
		{
			WebBrowserExtensions.ThrowExceptionIfFrameNull(frame);
			if (timeout.HasValue && timeout.Value.TotalMilliseconds > 4294967295.0)
			{
				throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("timeout", "Timeout greater than Maximum allowable value of " + uint.MaxValue);
			}
			JavascriptResponse javascriptResponse = await frame.EvaluateScriptAsync(script, "about:blank", 1, timeout).ConfigureAwait(continueOnCapturedContext: false);
			if (javascriptResponse.Success)
			{
				IBinder instance = DefaultBinder.Instance;
				return (T)instance.Bind(javascriptResponse.Result, typeof(T));
			}
			throw new Exception(javascriptResponse.Message);
		}

		public static Task<T> EvaluateScriptAsync<T>(this IBrowser browser, string script, TimeSpan? timeout = null)
		{
			WebBrowserExtensions.ThrowExceptionIfBrowserNull(browser);
			using IFrame frame = browser.MainFrame;
			return frame.EvaluateScriptAsync<T>(script, timeout);
		}

		public static Task<T> EvaluateScriptAsync<T>(this IChromiumWebBrowserBase chromiumWebBrowser, string script, TimeSpan? timeout = null)
		{
			WebBrowserExtensions.ThrowExceptionIfChromiumWebBrowserDisposed(chromiumWebBrowser);
			if (chromiumWebBrowser is IWebBrowser { CanExecuteJavascriptInMainFrame: false })
			{
				WebBrowserExtensions.ThrowExceptionIfCanExecuteJavascriptInMainFrameFalse();
			}
			return chromiumWebBrowser.BrowserCore.EvaluateScriptAsync<T>(script, timeout);
		}
	}

		public int Y
		{
			get

			}
			set

			}
		}

		public int Width
		{
			get
			{
				return windowInfo.Width;
			}
			set
			{
				windowInfo.Width = value;
			}
		}

		public int Height
		{
			get
			{
				return windowInfo.Height;
			}
			set
			{
				windowInfo.Height = value;
			}
		}

		public uint Style
		{
			get
			{
				return windowInfo.Style;
			}
			set
			{
				windowInfo.Style = value;
			}
		}

		public uint ExStyle
		{
			get
			{
				return windowInfo.ExStyle;
			}
			set
			{
				windowInfo.ExStyle = value;
			}
		}

		public IntPtr ParentWindowHandle
		{
			get
			{
				return windowInfo.ParentWindowHandle;
			}
			set
			{
				windowInfo.ParentWindowHandle = value;
			}
		}

		public bool WindowlessRenderingEnabled
		{
			get
			{
				return windowInfo.WindowlessRenderingEnabled;
			}
			set
			{
				windowInfo.WindowlessRenderingEnabled = value;
			}
		}

		public bool SharedTextureEnabled
		{
			get
			{
				return windowInfo.SharedTextureEnabled;
			}
			set
			{
				windowInfo.SharedTextureEnabled = value;
			}
		}

		public bool ExternalBeginFrameEnabled
		{
			get
			{
				return windowInfo.ExternalBeginFrameEnabled;
			}
			set
			{
				windowInfo.ExternalBeginFrameEnabled = value;
			}
		}

		public IntPtr WindowHandle
		{
			get
			{
				return windowInfo.WindowHandle;
			}
			set
			{
				windowInfo.WindowHandle = value;
			}
		}

		public string WindowName
		{
			get
			{
				return windowInfo.WindowName;
			}
			set
			{
				windowInfo.WindowName = value;
			}
		}

		public void Dispose()
		{
			windowInfo.Dispose();
		}

		public void SetAsChild(IntPtr parentHandle)
		{
			windowInfo.SetAsChild(parentHandle);
		}

		public void SetAsChild(IntPtr parentHandle, CefSharp.Structs.Rect windowBounds)
		{
			windowInfo.SetAsChild(parentHandle, windowBounds);
		}

		public void SetAsChild(IntPtr parentHandle, int left, int top, int right, int bottom)
		{
			windowInfo.SetAsChild(parentHandle, left, top, right, bottom);
		}

		public void SetAsPopup(IntPtr parentHandle, string windowName)
		{
			windowInfo.SetAsPopup(parentHandle, windowName);
		}

		public void SetAsWindowless(IntPtr parentHandle)
		{
			windowInfo.SetAsWindowless(parentHandle);
		}

		public static IWindowInfo Create()
		{
			return new CefSharp.Core.WindowInfo();
		}

		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		public IWindowInfo UnWrap()
		{
			return windowInfo;
		}
	}

namespace CefSharp.Core

	public static class ObjectFactory
	{
		public static readonly Type BrowserSetingsType = typeof(BrowserSettings);

		public static readonly Type RequestContextType = typeof(RequestContext);

		public static IBrowserSettings CreateBrowserSettings(bool autoDispose)
		{
			return new BrowserSettings(autoDispose);
		}

		public static IWindowInfo CreateWindowInfo()
		{
			return new WindowInfo();
		}

		public static IPostData CreatePostData()
		{
			return new PostData();
		}

		public static IPostDataElement CreatePostDataElement()
		{
			return new PostDataElement();
		}

		public static IRequest CreateRequest()
		{
			return new Request();
		}

		public static IUrlRequest CreateUrlRequest(IRequest request, IUrlRequestClient urlRequestClient)
		{
			return new UrlRequest(request, urlRequestClient);
		}

		public static IUrlRequest CreateUrlRequest(IRequest request, IUrlRequestClient urlRequestClient, IRequestContext requestContext)
		{
			return new UrlRequest(request, urlRequestClient, requestContext);
		}

		public static IDragData CreateDragData()
		{
			return DragData.Create();
		}

		public static RequestContextBuilder ConfigureRequestContext()
		{
			return new RequestContextBuilder();
		}
	}

namespace CefSharp.Fluent
{
	public delegate bool CanDownloadDelegate(IWebBrowser chromiumWebBrowser, IBrowser browser, string url, string requestMethod);
	public delegate void OnBeforeDownloadDelegate(IWebBrowser chromiumWebBrowser, IBrowser browser, DownloadItem downloadItem, IBeforeDownloadCallback callback);
	public delegate void OnDownloadUpdatedDelegate(IWebBrowser chromiumWebBrowser, IBrowser browser, DownloadItem downloadItem, IDownloadItemCallback callback);
	public class DownloadHandler : CefSharp.Handler.DownloadHandler
	{
		private CanDownloadDelegate canDownload;

		private OnBeforeDownloadDelegate onBeforeDownload;

		private OnDownloadUpdatedDelegate onDownloadUpdated;

		public static DownloadHandlerBuilder Create()
		{
			return new DownloadHandlerBuilder();
		}

		public static IDownloadHandler UseFolder(string folder, OnDownloadUpdatedDelegate downloadUpdated = null)
		{
			return Create().OnBeforeDownload(delegate(IWebBrowser chromiumWebBrowser, IBrowser browser, DownloadItem item, IBeforeDownloadCallback callback)
			{
				using (callback)
				{
					string downloadPath = Path.Combine(folder, item.SuggestedFileName);
					callback.Continue(downloadPath, showDialog: false);
				}
			}).OnDownloadUpdated(downloadUpdated).Build();
		}

		public static IDownloadHandler AskUser(OnDownloadUpdatedDelegate downloadUpdated = null)
		{
			return Create().OnBeforeDownload(delegate(IWebBrowser chromiumWebBrowser, IBrowser browser, DownloadItem item, IBeforeDownloadCallback callback)
			{
				using (callback)
				{
					callback.Continue("", showDialog: true);
				}
			}).OnDownloadUpdated(downloadUpdated).Build();
		}

		internal DownloadHandler()
		{
		}

		internal void SetCanDownload(CanDownloadDelegate action)
		{
			canDownload = action;
		}

		internal void SetOnBeforeDownload(OnBeforeDownloadDelegate action)
		{
			onBeforeDownload = action;
		}

		internal void SetOnDownloadUpdated(OnDownloadUpdatedDelegate action)
		{
			onDownloadUpdated = action;
		}

		protected override bool CanDownload(IWebBrowser chromiumWebBrowser, IBrowser browser, string url, string requestMethod)
		{
			return canDownload?.Invoke(chromiumWebBrowser, browser, url, requestMethod) ?? true;
		}

		protected override void OnBeforeDownload(IWebBrowser chromiumWebBrowser, IBrowser browser, DownloadItem downloadItem, IBeforeDownloadCallback callback)
		{
			onBeforeDownload?.Invoke(chromiumWebBrowser, browser, downloadItem, callback);
		}

		protected override void OnDownloadUpdated(IWebBrowser chromiumWebBrowser, IBrowser browser, DownloadItem downloadItem, IDownloadItemCallback callback)
		{
			onDownloadUpdated?.Invoke(chromiumWebBrowser, browser, downloadItem, callback);
		}
	}
	public class DownloadHandlerBuilder
	{
		private readonly DownloadHandler handler = new DownloadHandler();

		public DownloadHandlerBuilder CanDownload(CanDownloadDelegate action)
		{
			handler.SetCanDownload(action);
			return this;
		}

		public DownloadHandlerBuilder OnBeforeDownload(OnBeforeDownloadDelegate action)
		{
			handler.SetOnBeforeDownload(action);
			return this;
		}

		public DownloadHandlerBuilder OnDownloadUpdated(OnDownloadUpdatedDelegate action)
		{
			handler.SetOnDownloadUpdated(action);
			return this;
		}

		public IDownloadHandler Build()
		{
			return handler;
		}
	}
	public delegate bool GetAuthCredentialsDelegate(bool isProxy, string host, int port, string realm, string scheme, IAuthCallback callback);
	public delegate void OnDownloadDataDelegate(IUrlRequest request, Stream data);
	public delegate void OnDownloadProgressDelegate(IUrlRequest request, long current, long total);
	public delegate void OnRequestCompleteDelegate(IUrlRequest request);
	public delegate void OnUploadProgressDelegate(IUrlRequest request, long current, long total);
	public class UrlRequestClient : CefSharp.UrlRequestClient
	{
		private GetAuthCredentialsDelegate getAuthCredentials;

		private OnDownloadDataDelegate onDownloadData;

		private OnDownloadProgressDelegate onDownloadProgress;

		private OnRequestCompleteDelegate onRequestComplete;

		private OnUploadProgressDelegate onUploadProgress;

		public static UrlRequestClientBuilder Create()
		{
			return new UrlRequestClientBuilder();
		}

		internal UrlRequestClient()
		{
		}

		internal void SetGetAuthCredentials(GetAuthCredentialsDelegate func)
		{
			getAuthCredentials = func;
		}

		internal void SetOnDownloadData(OnDownloadDataDelegate action)
		{
			onDownloadData = action;
		}

		internal void SetOnDownloadProgress(OnDownloadProgressDelegate action)
		{
			onDownloadProgress = action;
		}

		internal void SetOnRequestComplete(OnRequestCompleteDelegate action)
		{
			onRequestComplete = action;
		}

		internal void SetOnUploadProgress(OnUploadProgressDelegate action)
		{
			onUploadProgress = action;
		}

		protected override bool GetAuthCredentials(bool isProxy, string host, int port, string realm, string scheme, IAuthCallback callback)
		{
			return getAuthCredentials?.Invoke(isProxy, host, port, realm, scheme, callback) ?? false;
		}

		protected override void OnDownloadData(IUrlRequest request, Stream data)
		{
			onDownloadData?.Invoke(request, data);
		}

		protected override void OnDownloadProgress(IUrlRequest request, long current, long total)
		{
			onDownloadProgress?.Invoke(request, current, total);
		}

		protected override void OnRequestComplete(IUrlRequest request)
		{
			onRequestComplete?.Invoke(request);
		}

		protected override void OnUploadProgress(IUrlRequest request, long current, long total)
		{
			onUploadProgress?.Invoke(request, current, total);
		}
	}
	public class UrlRequestClientBuilder
	{
		private UrlRequestClient client = new UrlRequestClient();

		public UrlRequestClientBuilder GetAuthCredentials(GetAuthCredentialsDelegate func)
		{
			client.SetGetAuthCredentials(func);
			return this;
		}

		public UrlRequestClientBuilder OnDownloadData(OnDownloadDataDelegate action)
		{
			client.SetOnDownloadData(action);
			return this;
		}

		public UrlRequestClientBuilder OnDownloadProgress(OnDownloadProgressDelegate action)
		{
			client.SetOnDownloadProgress(action);
			return this;
		}

		public UrlRequestClientBuilder OnRequestComplete(OnRequestCompleteDelegate action)
		{
			client.SetOnRequestComplete(action);
			return this;
		}

		public UrlRequestClientBuilder OnUploadProgress(OnUploadProgressDelegate action)
		{
			client.SetOnUploadProgress(action);
			return this;
		}

		public IUrlRequestClient Build()
		{
			return client;
		}
	}
}

		public event EventHandler<DevToolsEventArgs> DevToolsEvent;

		public event EventHandler<DevToolsErrorEventArgs> DevToolsEventError;

		public DevToolsClient(IBrowser browser)
		{
			this.browser = browser;
			CaptureSyncContext = true;
		}

		public void SetDevToolsObserverRegistration(IRegistration devToolsRegistration)
		{
			this.devToolsRegistration = devToolsRegistration;
		}

		public void AddEventHandler<T>(string eventName, EventHandler<T> eventHandler) where T : EventArgs
		{
			IEventProxy orAdd = eventHandlers.GetOrAdd(eventName, (string _) => new EventProxy<T>(DeserializeJsonEvent<T>));
			EventProxy<T> eventProxy = (EventProxy<T>)orAdd;
			eventProxy.AddHandler(eventHandler);
		}

		public bool RemoveEventHandler<T>(string eventName, EventHandler<T> eventHandler) where T : EventArgs
		{
			if (eventHandlers.TryGetValue(eventName, out var value))
			{
				EventProxy<T> eventProxy = (EventProxy<T>)value;
				IEventProxy value2;
				if (eventProxy.RemoveHandler(eventHandler))
				{
					return !eventHandlers.TryRemove(eventName, out value2);
				}
			}
			return true;
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync(string method, IDictionary<string, object> parameters = null)
		{
			return ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>(method, parameters);
		}

		public Task<T> ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<T>(string method, IDictionary<string, object> parameters = null) where T : DevToolsDomainResponseBase
		{
			if (browser == null || browser.IsDisposed)
			{
				throw new ObjectDisposedException("IBrowser");
			}
			TaskCompletionSource<T> taskCompletionSource = new TaskCompletionSource<T>();
			DevToolsMethodResponseContext methodResultContext = new DevToolsMethodResponseContext(typeof(T), (object o) => taskCompletionSource.TrySetResult((T)o), taskCompletionSource.TrySetException, CaptureSyncContext ? SynchronizationContext.Current : SyncContext);
			IBrowserHost browserHost = browser.GetHost();
			int messageId = browserHost.GetNextDevToolsMessageId();
			if (!queuedCommandResults.TryAdd(messageId, methodResultContext))
			{
				throw new DevToolsClientException($"Unable to add MessageId {messageId} to queuedCommandResults ConcurrentDictionary.");
			}
			if (CefThread.CurrentlyOnUiThread)
			{
				ExecuteDevToolsMethod(browserHost, messageId, method, parameters, methodResultContext);
			}
			else
			{
				if (!CefThread.CanExecuteOnUiThread)
				{
					queuedCommandResults.TryRemove(messageId, out methodResultContext);
					throw new DevToolsClientException("Unable to invoke ExecuteDevToolsMethod on CEF UI Thread.");
				}
				CefThread.ExecuteOnUiThread(delegate
				{
					ExecuteDevToolsMethod(browserHost, messageId, method, parameters, methodResultContext);
				});
			}
			return taskCompletionSource.Task;
		}

		private void ExecuteDevToolsMethod(IBrowserHost browserHost, int messageId, string method, IDictionary<string, object> parameters, DevToolsMethodResponseContext methodResultContext)
		{
			try
			{
				int num = browserHost.ExecuteDevToolsMethod(messageId, method, parameters);
				if (num == 0)
				{
					throw new DevToolsClientException($"Failed to execute dev tools method {method}.");
				}
				if (num != messageId)
				{
					throw new DevToolsClientException($"Generated MessageId {num} doesn't match returned Message Id {messageId}");
				}
			}
			catch (Exception exception)
			{
				queuedCommandResults.TryRemove(messageId, out var _);
				methodResultContext.SetException(exception);
			}
		}

		public void Dispose()
		{
			if (Interlocked.Increment(ref disposeCount) != 1)
			{
				return;
			}
			this.DevToolsEvent = null;
			devToolsRegistration?.Dispose();
			devToolsRegistration = null;
			browser = null;
			ICollection<IEventProxy> values = eventHandlers.Values;
			eventHandlers.Clear();
			foreach (IEventProxy item in values)
			{
				item.Dispose();
			}
		}

		void IDevToolsMessageObserver.OnDevToolsAgentAttached(IBrowser browser)
		{
			devToolsAttached = true;
		}

		void IDevToolsMessageObserver.OnDevToolsAgentDetached(IBrowser browser)
		{
			devToolsAttached = false;
		}

		void IDevToolsMessageObserver.OnDevToolsEvent(IBrowser browser, string method, Stream parameters)
		{
			try
			{
				EventHandler<DevToolsEventArgs> eventHandler = this.DevToolsEvent;
				if (eventHandler != null)
				{
					string paramsAsJsonString = StreamToString(parameters, leaveOpen: true);
					eventHandler(this, new DevToolsEventArgs(method, paramsAsJsonString));
				}
				if (eventHandlers.TryGetValue(method, out var value))
				{
					value.Raise(this, method, parameters, SyncContext);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				EventHandler<DevToolsErrorEventArgs> eventHandler2 = this.DevToolsEventError;
				string json = "";
				if (parameters.Length > 0)
				{
					parameters.Position = 0L;
					try
					{
						json = StreamToString(parameters);
					}
					catch (Exception)
					{
					}
				}
				DevToolsErrorEventArgs e = new DevToolsErrorEventArgs(method, json, ex);
				eventHandler2?.Invoke(this, e);
			}
		}

		bool IDevToolsMessageObserver.OnDevToolsMessage(IBrowser browser, Stream message)
		{
			return false;
		}

		void IDevToolsMessageObserver.OnDevToolsMethodResult(IBrowser browser, int messageId, bool success, Stream result)
		{
			if (!queuedCommandResults.TryRemove(messageId, out var value))
			{
				return;
			}
			if (success)
			{
				if (!(value.Type == typeof(DevToolsMethodResponse)) && !(value.Type == typeof(DevToolsDomainResponseBase)))
				{
					try
					{
						value.SetResult(DeserializeJson(value.Type, result));
						return;
					}
					catch (Exception exception)
					{
						value.SetException(exception);
						return;
					}
				}
				value.SetResult(new DevToolsMethodResponse
				{
					Success = success,
					MessageId = messageId,
					ResponseAsJsonString = StreamToString(result)
				});
			}
			else
			{
				DevToolsDomainErrorResponse devToolsDomainErrorResponse = DeserializeJson<DevToolsDomainErrorResponse>(result);
				devToolsDomainErrorResponse.MessageId = messageId;
				value.SetException(new DevToolsClientException("DevTools Client Error :" + devToolsDomainErrorResponse.Message, devToolsDomainErrorResponse));
			}
		}

		private static T DeserializeJsonEvent<T>(string eventName, Stream stream) where T : EventArgs
		{
			if (typeof(T) == typeof(EventArgs))
			{
				return (T)EventArgs.Empty;
			}
			if (typeof(T) == typeof(DevToolsEventArgs))
			{
				string paramsAsJsonString = StreamToString(stream, leaveOpen: true);
				DevToolsEventArgs e = new DevToolsEventArgs(eventName, paramsAsJsonString);
				return (T)(EventArgs)e;
			}
			return (T)DeserializeJson(typeof(T), stream);
		}

		private static T DeserializeJson<T>(Stream stream)
		{
			return (T)DeserializeJson(typeof(T), stream);
		}

		private static object DeserializeJson(Type type, Stream stream)
		{
			DataContractJsonSerializerSettings dataContractJsonSerializerSettings = new DataContractJsonSerializerSettings();
			dataContractJsonSerializerSettings.UseSimpleDictionaryFormat = true;
			DataContractJsonSerializer dataContractJsonSerializer = new DataContractJsonSerializer(type, dataContractJsonSerializerSettings);
			return dataContractJsonSerializer.ReadObject(stream);
		}

		private static string StreamToString(Stream stream, bool leaveOpen = false)
		{
			using StreamReader streamReader = new StreamReader(stream, Encoding.UTF8, detectEncodingFromByteOrderMarks: false, 1024, leaveOpen);
			return streamReader.ReadToEnd();
		}
	}
	[Serializable]
	public class DevToolsClientException : Exception
	{
		public DevToolsDomainErrorResponse Response { get; private set; }

		public DevToolsClientException()
			: base("Error occurred whilst executing DevTools protocol method")
		{
		}

		public DevToolsClientException(string message)
			: base(message)
		{
		}

		public DevToolsClientException(string message, DevToolsDomainErrorResponse errorResponse)
			: base(message)
		{
			Response = errorResponse;
		}

		public DevToolsClientException(string message, Exception inner)
			: base(message, inner)
		{
		}

		protected DevToolsClientException(SerializationInfo serializationInfo, StreamingContext streamingContext)
			: base(serializationInfo, streamingContext)
		{
		}
	}
	public abstract class DevToolsDomainBase
	{
		protected string EnumToString(Enum val)
		{
			MemberInfo[] member = val.GetType().GetMember(val.ToString());
			EnumMemberAttribute enumMemberAttribute = (EnumMemberAttribute)Attribute.GetCustomAttribute(member[0], typeof(EnumMemberAttribute), inherit: false);
			return enumMemberAttribute.Value;
		}

		protected IEnumerable<string> EnumToString(ContentEncoding[] values)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < values.Length; i++)
			{
				ContentEncoding contentEncoding = values[i];
				MemberInfo[] member = contentEncoding.GetType().GetMember(contentEncoding.ToString());
				EnumMemberAttribute enumMemberAttribute = (EnumMemberAttribute)Attribute.GetCustomAttribute(member[0], typeof(EnumMemberAttribute), inherit: false);
				yield return enumMemberAttribute.Value;
			}
		}

		protected IEnumerable<string> EnumToString(PermissionType[] values)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < values.Length; i++)
			{
				PermissionType permissionType = values[i];
				MemberInfo[] member = permissionType.GetType().GetMember(permissionType.ToString());
				EnumMemberAttribute enumMemberAttribute = (EnumMemberAttribute)Attribute.GetCustomAttribute(member[0], typeof(EnumMemberAttribute), inherit: false);
				yield return enumMemberAttribute.Value;
			}
		}

		protected IEnumerable<string> EnumToString(DisabledImageType[] values)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < values.Length; i++)
			{
				DisabledImageType disabledImageType = values[i];
				MemberInfo[] member = disabledImageType.GetType().GetMember(disabledImageType.ToString());
				EnumMemberAttribute enumMemberAttribute = (EnumMemberAttribute)Attribute.GetCustomAttribute(member[0], typeof(EnumMemberAttribute), inherit: false);
				yield return enumMemberAttribute.Value;
			}
		}

		protected IEnumerable<string> EnumToString(CSPViolationType[] values)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < values.Length; i++)
			{
				CSPViolationType cSPViolationType = values[i];
				MemberInfo[] member = cSPViolationType.GetType().GetMember(cSPViolationType.ToString());
				EnumMemberAttribute enumMemberAttribute = (EnumMemberAttribute)Attribute.GetCustomAttribute(member[0], typeof(EnumMemberAttribute), inherit: false);
				yield return enumMemberAttribute.Value;
			}
		}

		protected string ToBase64String(byte[] bytes)
		{
			return Convert.ToBase64String(bytes);
		}
	}
	[DataContract]
	public abstract class DevToolsDomainEntityBase
	{
		internal static object StringToEnum(Type enumType, string input)
		{
			if (enumType.IsArray)
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(input) || input == "[]" || input == "[ ]")
				{
					return null;
				}
				string[] array = input.Substring(1, input.Length - 2).Split(',');
				Array array2 = Array.CreateInstance(enumType.GetElementType(), array.Length);
				for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
				{
					string input2 = array[i].Trim('\r', '\n', '"', ' ');
					object value = StringToEnumInternal(enumType.GetElementType(), input2);
					array2.SetValue(value, i);
				}
				return array2;
			}
			if (enumType.IsGenericType && enumType.GetGenericTypeDefinition() == typeof(Nullable<>))
			{
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(input))
				{
					return null;
				}
				enumType = Nullable.GetUnderlyingType(enumType);
			}
			return StringToEnumInternal(enumType, input);
		}

		private static object StringToEnumInternal(Type enumType, string input)
		{
			string[] names = Enum.GetNames(enumType);
			foreach (string text in names)
			{
				EnumMemberAttribute enumMemberAttribute = ((EnumMemberAttribute[])enumType.GetField(text).GetCustomAttributes(typeof(EnumMemberAttribute), inherit: true)).Single();
				if (enumMemberAttribute.Value == input)
				{
					return Enum.Parse(enumType, text);
				}
			}
			return Enum.GetValues(enumType).GetValue(0);
		}

		internal static string EnumToString(Enum e)
		{
			MemberInfo element = e.GetType().GetMember(e.ToString()).FirstOrDefault();
			EnumMemberAttribute enumMemberAttribute = (EnumMemberAttribute)Attribute.GetCustomAttribute(element, typeof(EnumMemberAttribute), inherit: false);
			return enumMemberAttribute.Value;
		}

		internal static string EnumToString(Array enumArray)
		{
			string text = "[";
			foreach (object item in enumArray)
			{
				MemberInfo element = item.GetType().GetMember(item.ToString()).FirstOrDefault();
				EnumMemberAttribute enumMemberAttribute = (EnumMemberAttribute)Attribute.GetCustomAttribute(element, typeof(EnumMemberAttribute), inherit: false);
				text = text + enumMemberAttribute.Value + ",";
			}
			return text + text.Substring(0, text.Length - 1) + "]";
		}

		public IDictionary<string, object> ToDictionary()
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			PropertyInfo[] properties = GetType().GetProperties(BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.Public | BindingFlags.NonPublic);
			PropertyInfo[] array = properties;
			foreach (PropertyInfo propertyInfo in array)
			{
				DataMemberAttribute dataMemberAttribute = (DataMemberAttribute)Attribute.GetCustomAttribute(propertyInfo, typeof(DataMemberAttribute), inherit: false);
				if (dataMemberAttribute == null)
				{
					continue;
				}
				string name = dataMemberAttribute.Name;
				bool isRequired = dataMemberAttribute.IsRequired;
				object obj = propertyInfo.GetValue(this);
				if (isRequired && obj == null)
				{
					throw new DevToolsClientException(propertyInfo.Name + " is required");
				}
				if (obj == null)
				{
					continue;
				}
				Type type = obj.GetType();
				if (typeof(DevToolsDomainEntityBase).IsAssignableFrom(type))
				{
					obj = ((DevToolsDomainEntityBase)obj).ToDictionary();
				}
				else if (propertyInfo.PropertyType.IsGenericType && propertyInfo.PropertyType.GetGenericTypeDefinition() == typeof(IList<>) && typeof(DevToolsDomainEntityBase).IsAssignableFrom(propertyInfo.PropertyType.GetGenericArguments()[0]))
				{
					List<IDictionary<string, object>> list = new List<IDictionary<string, object>>();
					foreach (object item in (IEnumerable)obj)
					{
						list.Add(((DevToolsDomainEntityBase)item).ToDictionary());
					}
					obj = list;
				}
				dictionary.Add(name, obj);
			}
			return dictionary;
		}
	}

	}

		}
	}

	public class DevToolsMethodResponse : DevToolsDomainResponseBase
	{
		public int MessageId { get; set; }

		public bool Success { get; set; }

		public string ResponseAsJsonString { get; set; }
	}
	internal struct DevToolsMethodResponseContext
	{
		public readonly Type Type;

		private readonly Func<object, bool> setResult;

		private readonly Func<Exception, bool> setException;

		private readonly SynchronizationContext syncContext;

		public DevToolsMethodResponseContext(Type type, Func<object, bool> setResult, Func<Exception, bool> setException, SynchronizationContext syncContext)
		{
			Type = type;
			this.setResult = setResult;
			this.setException = setException;
			this.syncContext = syncContext;
		}

		public void SetResult(object result)
		{
			InvokeOnSyncContext(setResult, result);
		}

		public void SetException(Exception ex)
		{
			InvokeOnSyncContext(setException, ex);
		}

		private void InvokeOnSyncContext<T>(Func<T, bool> fn, T value)
		{
			if (syncContext == null || syncContext == SynchronizationContext.Current)
			{
				fn(value);
				return;
			}
			syncContext.Post(delegate(object state)
			{
				KeyValuePair<Func<T, bool>, T> keyValuePair = (KeyValuePair<Func<T, bool>, T>)state;
				keyValuePair.Key(keyValuePair.Value);
			}, new KeyValuePair<Func<T, bool>, T>(fn, value));
		}
	}
	internal class EventProxy<T> : IEventProxy, IDisposable
	{
		private Func<string, Stream, T> convert;

		private event EventHandler<T> handlers;

		public EventProxy(Func<string, Stream, T> convert)
		{
			this.convert = convert;
		}

		public void AddHandler(EventHandler<T> handler)
		{
			handlers += handler;
		}

		public bool RemoveHandler(EventHandler<T> handler)
		{
			handlers -= handler;
			return this.handlers == null;
		}

		public void Raise(object sender, string eventName, Stream stream, SynchronizationContext syncContext)
		{
			stream.Position = 0L;
			T args = convert(eventName, stream);
			if (syncContext == null)
			{
				this.handlers?.Invoke(sender, args);
				return;
			}
			syncContext.Post(delegate
			{
				this.handlers?.Invoke(sender, args);
			}, null);
		}

		public void Dispose()
		{
			this.handlers = null;
		}
	}
	public interface IDevToolsClient : IDisposable
	{
		event EventHandler<DevToolsEventArgs> DevToolsEvent;

		event EventHandler<DevToolsErrorEventArgs> DevToolsEventError;

		void AddEventHandler<T>(string eventName, EventHandler<T> eventHandler) where T : EventArgs;

		bool RemoveEventHandler<T>(string eventName, EventHandler<T> eventHandler) where T : EventArgs;

		Task<T> ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<T>(string method, IDictionary<string, object> parameters = null) where T : DevToolsDomainResponseBase;
	}
	internal interface IEventProxy : IDisposable
	{
		void Raise(object sender, string eventName, Stream stream, SynchronizationContext syncContext);
	}

	public class IOClient : DevToolsDomainBase
	{
		private IDevToolsClient _client;

		public IOClient(IDevToolsClient client)
		{
			_client = client;
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> CloseAsync(string handle)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("handle", handle);
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("IO.close", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<ReadResponse> ReadAsync(string handle, int? offset = null, int? size = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("handle", handle);
			if (offset.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("offset", offset.Value);
			}
			if (size.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("size", size.Value);
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<ReadResponse>("IO.read", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<ResolveBlobResponse> ResolveBlobAsync(string objectId)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("objectId", objectId);
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<ResolveBlobResponse>("IO.resolveBlob", dictionary);
		}
	}

	public enum RemoteObjectType
	{
		[EnumMember(Value = "object")]
		Object,
		[EnumMember(Value = "function")]

	public class RuntimeClient : DevToolsDomainBase
	{
		private IDevToolsClient _client;

		public event EventHandler<BindingCalledEventArgs> BindingCalled
		{
			add
			{
				_client.AddEventHandler("Runtime.bindingCalled", value);
			}
			remove
			{
				_client.RemoveEventHandler("Runtime.bindingCalled", value);
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler<ConsoleAPICalledEventArgs> ConsoleAPICalled
		{
			add
			{
				_client.AddEventHandler("Runtime.consoleAPICalled", value);
			}
			remove
			{
				_client.RemoveEventHandler("Runtime.consoleAPICalled", value);
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler<ExceptionRevokedEventArgs> ExceptionRevoked
		{
			add
			{
				_client.AddEventHandler("Runtime.exceptionRevoked", value);
			}
			remove
			{
				_client.RemoveEventHandler("Runtime.exceptionRevoked", value);
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler<ExceptionThrownEventArgs> ExceptionThrown
		{
			add
			{
				_client.AddEventHandler("Runtime.exceptionThrown", value);
			}
			remove
			{
				_client.RemoveEventHandler("Runtime.exceptionThrown", value);
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler<ExecutionContextCreatedEventArgs> ExecutionContextCreated
		{
			add
			{
				_client.AddEventHandler("Runtime.executionContextCreated", value);
			}
			remove
			{
				_client.RemoveEventHandler("Runtime.executionContextCreated", value);
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler<ExecutionContextDestroyedEventArgs> ExecutionContextDestroyed
		{
			add
			{
				_client.AddEventHandler("Runtime.executionContextDestroyed", value);
			}
			remove
			{
				_client.RemoveEventHandler("Runtime.executionContextDestroyed", value);
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler<EventArgs> ExecutionContextsCleared
		{
			add
			{
				_client.AddEventHandler("Runtime.executionContextsCleared", value);
			}
			remove
			{
				_client.RemoveEventHandler("Runtime.executionContextsCleared", value);
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler<InspectRequestedEventArgs> InspectRequested
		{
			add
			{
				_client.AddEventHandler("Runtime.inspectRequested", value);
			}
			remove
			{
				_client.RemoveEventHandler("Runtime.inspectRequested", value);
			}
		}

		public RuntimeClient(IDevToolsClient client)

		public Task<AwaitPromiseResponse> AwaitPromiseAsync(string promiseObjectId, bool? returnByValue = null, bool? generatePreview = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("promiseObjectId", promiseObjectId);
			if (returnByValue.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("returnByValue", returnByValue.Value);
			}
			if (generatePreview.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("generatePreview", generatePreview.Value);
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<AwaitPromiseResponse>("Runtime.awaitPromise", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<CallFunctionOnResponse> CallFunctionOnAsync(string functionDeclaration, string objectId = null, IList<CallArgument> arguments = null, bool? silent = null, bool? returnByValue = null, bool? generatePreview = null, bool? userGesture = null, bool? awaitPromise = null, int? executionContextId = null, string objectGroup = null, bool? throwOnSideEffect = null, string uniqueContextId = null, bool? generateWebDriverValue = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("functionDeclaration", functionDeclaration);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(objectId))
			{
				dictionary.Add("objectId", objectId);
			}
			if (arguments != null)
			{
				dictionary.Add("arguments", arguments.Select((CallArgument x) => x.ToDictionary()));
			}
			if (silent.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("silent", silent.Value);
			}
			if (returnByValue.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("returnByValue", returnByValue.Value);
			}
			if (generatePreview.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("generatePreview", generatePreview.Value);
			}
			if (userGesture.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("userGesture", userGesture.Value);
			}
			if (awaitPromise.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("awaitPromise", awaitPromise.Value);
			}
			if (executionContextId.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("executionContextId", executionContextId.Value);
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(objectGroup))
			{
				dictionary.Add("objectGroup", objectGroup);
			}
			if (throwOnSideEffect.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("throwOnSideEffect", throwOnSideEffect.Value);
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(uniqueContextId))
			{
				dictionary.Add("uniqueContextId", uniqueContextId);
			}
			if (generateWebDriverValue.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("generateWebDriverValue", generateWebDriverValue.Value);
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<CallFunctionOnResponse>("Runtime.callFunctionOn", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<EvaluateResponse> EvaluateAsync(string expression, string objectGroup = null, bool? includeCommandLineAPI = null, bool? silent = null, int? contextId = null, bool? returnByValue = null, bool? generatePreview = null, bool? userGesture = null, bool? awaitPromise = null, bool? throwOnSideEffect = null, double? timeout = null, bool? disableBreaks = null, bool? replMode = null, bool? allowUnsafeEvalBlockedByCSP = null, string uniqueContextId = null, bool? generateWebDriverValue = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("expression", expression);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(objectGroup))
			{
				dictionary.Add("objectGroup", objectGroup);
			}
			if (includeCommandLineAPI.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("includeCommandLineAPI", includeCommandLineAPI.Value);
			}
			if (silent.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("silent", silent.Value);
			}
			if (contextId.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("contextId", contextId.Value);
			}
			if (returnByValue.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("returnByValue", returnByValue.Value);
			}
			if (generatePreview.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("generatePreview", generatePreview.Value);
			}
			if (userGesture.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("userGesture", userGesture.Value);
			}
			if (awaitPromise.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("awaitPromise", awaitPromise.Value);
			}
			if (throwOnSideEffect.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("throwOnSideEffect", throwOnSideEffect.Value);
			}
			if (timeout.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("timeout", timeout.Value);
			}
			if (disableBreaks.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("disableBreaks", disableBreaks.Value);
			}
			if (replMode.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("replMode", replMode.Value);
			}
			if (allowUnsafeEvalBlockedByCSP.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("allowUnsafeEvalBlockedByCSP", allowUnsafeEvalBlockedByCSP.Value);
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(uniqueContextId))
			{
				dictionary.Add("uniqueContextId", uniqueContextId);
			}
			if (generateWebDriverValue.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("generateWebDriverValue", generateWebDriverValue.Value);
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<EvaluateResponse>("Runtime.evaluate", dictionary);
		}

	public class ProfilerClient : DevToolsDomainBase
	{
		private IDevToolsClient _client;

		public event EventHandler<ConsoleProfileFinishedEventArgs> ConsoleProfileFinished
		{
			add
			{
				_client.AddEventHandler("Profiler.consoleProfileFinished", value);
			}
			remove
			{
				_client.RemoveEventHandler("Profiler.consoleProfileFinished", value);
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler<ConsoleProfileStartedEventArgs> ConsoleProfileStarted
		{
			add
			{
				_client.AddEventHandler("Profiler.consoleProfileStarted", value);
			}
			remove
			{
				_client.RemoveEventHandler("Profiler.consoleProfileStarted", value);
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler<PreciseCoverageDeltaUpdateEventArgs> PreciseCoverageDeltaUpdate
		{
			add
			{
				_client.AddEventHandler("Profiler.preciseCoverageDeltaUpdate", value);
			}
			remove
			{
				_client.RemoveEventHandler("Profiler.preciseCoverageDeltaUpdate", value);
			}
		}

		public ProfilerClient(IDevToolsClient client)
		{
			_client = client;
		}

	}
	public class HeapProfilerClient : DevToolsDomainBase
	{
		private IDevToolsClient _client;

		public event EventHandler<AddHeapSnapshotChunkEventArgs> AddHeapSnapshotChunk
		{
			add
			{
				_client.AddEventHandler("HeapProfiler.addHeapSnapshotChunk", value);
			}
			remove
			{
				_client.RemoveEventHandler("HeapProfiler.addHeapSnapshotChunk", value);
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler<HeapStatsUpdateEventArgs> HeapStatsUpdate
		{
			add
			{
				_client.AddEventHandler("HeapProfiler.heapStatsUpdate", value);
			}
			remove
			{
				_client.RemoveEventHandler("HeapProfiler.heapStatsUpdate", value);
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler<LastSeenObjectIdEventArgs> LastSeenObjectId
		{
			add
			{
				_client.AddEventHandler("HeapProfiler.lastSeenObjectId", value);
			}
			remove
			{
				_client.RemoveEventHandler("HeapProfiler.lastSeenObjectId", value);
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler<ReportHeapSnapshotProgressEventArgs> ReportHeapSnapshotProgress
		{
			add
			{
				_client.AddEventHandler("HeapProfiler.reportHeapSnapshotProgress", value);
			}
			remove
			{
				_client.RemoveEventHandler("HeapProfiler.reportHeapSnapshotProgress", value);
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler<EventArgs> ResetProfiles
		{
			add
			{
				_client.AddEventHandler("HeapProfiler.resetProfiles", value);
			}
			remove
			{
				_client.RemoveEventHandler("HeapProfiler.resetProfiles", value);
			}
		}

		public HeapProfilerClient(IDevToolsClient client)
		{
			_client = client;
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> AddInspectedHeapObjectAsync(string heapObjectId)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("heapObjectId", heapObjectId);
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("HeapProfiler.addInspectedHeapObject", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> CollectGarbageAsync()
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> parameters = null;
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("HeapProfiler.collectGarbage", parameters);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> DisableAsync()
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> parameters = null;
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("HeapProfiler.disable", parameters);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> EnableAsync()
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> parameters = null;
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("HeapProfiler.enable", parameters);
		}

		public Task<GetHeapObjectIdResponse> GetHeapObjectIdAsync(string objectId)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("objectId", objectId);
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<GetHeapObjectIdResponse>("HeapProfiler.getHeapObjectId", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<GetObjectByHeapObjectIdResponse> GetObjectByHeapObjectIdAsync(string objectId, string objectGroup = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("objectId", objectId);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(objectGroup))
			{
				dictionary.Add("objectGroup", objectGroup);
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<GetObjectByHeapObjectIdResponse>("HeapProfiler.getObjectByHeapObjectId", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<GetSamplingProfileResponse> GetSamplingProfileAsync()
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> parameters = null;
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<GetSamplingProfileResponse>("HeapProfiler.getSamplingProfile", parameters);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> StartSamplingAsync(double? samplingInterval = null, bool? includeObjectsCollectedByMajorGC = null, bool? includeObjectsCollectedByMinorGC = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			if (samplingInterval.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("samplingInterval", samplingInterval.Value);
			}
			if (includeObjectsCollectedByMajorGC.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("includeObjectsCollectedByMajorGC", includeObjectsCollectedByMajorGC.Value);
			}
			if (includeObjectsCollectedByMinorGC.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("includeObjectsCollectedByMinorGC", includeObjectsCollectedByMinorGC.Value);
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("HeapProfiler.startSampling", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> StartTrackingHeapObjectsAsync(bool? trackAllocations = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			if (trackAllocations.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("trackAllocations", trackAllocations.Value);
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("HeapProfiler.startTrackingHeapObjects", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<StopSamplingResponse> StopSamplingAsync()
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> parameters = null;
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<StopSamplingResponse>("HeapProfiler.stopSampling", parameters);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> StopTrackingHeapObjectsAsync(bool? reportProgress = null, bool? treatGlobalObjectsAsRoots = null, bool? captureNumericValue = null, bool? exposeInternals = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			if (reportProgress.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("reportProgress", reportProgress.Value);
			}
			if (treatGlobalObjectsAsRoots.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("treatGlobalObjectsAsRoots", treatGlobalObjectsAsRoots.Value);
			}
			if (captureNumericValue.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("captureNumericValue", captureNumericValue.Value);
			}
			if (exposeInternals.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("exposeInternals", exposeInternals.Value);
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("HeapProfiler.stopTrackingHeapObjects", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> TakeHeapSnapshotAsync(bool? reportProgress = null, bool? treatGlobalObjectsAsRoots = null, bool? captureNumericValue = null, bool? exposeInternals = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			if (reportProgress.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("reportProgress", reportProgress.Value);
			}
			if (treatGlobalObjectsAsRoots.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("treatGlobalObjectsAsRoots", treatGlobalObjectsAsRoots.Value);
			}
			if (captureNumericValue.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("captureNumericValue", captureNumericValue.Value);
			}
			if (exposeInternals.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("exposeInternals", exposeInternals.Value);
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("HeapProfiler.takeHeapSnapshot", dictionary);
		}
	}

		internal string status { get; set; }

		public string Status => status;

		[DataMember]
		internal ExceptionDetails exceptionDetails { get; set; }

		public ExceptionDetails ExceptionDetails => exceptionDetails;
	}

		public Task<EvaluateOnCallFrameResponse> EvaluateOnCallFrameAsync(string callFrameId, string expression, string objectGroup = null, bool? includeCommandLineAPI = null, bool? silent = null, bool? returnByValue = null, bool? generatePreview = null, bool? throwOnSideEffect = null, double? timeout = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("callFrameId", callFrameId);
			dictionary.Add("expression", expression);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(objectGroup))
			{
				dictionary.Add("objectGroup", objectGroup);
			}
			if (includeCommandLineAPI.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("includeCommandLineAPI", includeCommandLineAPI.Value);
			}
			if (silent.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("silent", silent.Value);
			}
			if (returnByValue.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("returnByValue", returnByValue.Value);
			}
			if (generatePreview.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("generatePreview", generatePreview.Value);
			}
			if (throwOnSideEffect.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("throwOnSideEffect", throwOnSideEffect.Value);
			}
			if (timeout.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("timeout", timeout.Value);
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<EvaluateOnCallFrameResponse>("Debugger.evaluateOnCallFrame", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<GetPossibleBreakpointsResponse> GetPossibleBreakpointsAsync(Location start, Location end = null, bool? restrictToFunction = null)

			{
				dictionary.Add("end", end.ToDictionary());

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> EnableAsync(bool? enableUI = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			if (enableUI.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("enableUI", enableUI.Value);
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("WebAuthn.enable", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> DisableAsync()
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> parameters = null;
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("WebAuthn.disable", parameters);
		}

		public Task<AddVirtualAuthenticatorResponse> AddVirtualAuthenticatorAsync(VirtualAuthenticatorOptions options)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("options", options.ToDictionary());
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<AddVirtualAuthenticatorResponse>("WebAuthn.addVirtualAuthenticator", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> SetResponseOverrideBitsAsync(string authenticatorId, bool? isBogusSignature = null, bool? isBadUV = null, bool? isBadUP = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("authenticatorId", authenticatorId);
			if (isBogusSignature.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("isBogusSignature", isBogusSignature.Value);
			}
			if (isBadUV.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("isBadUV", isBadUV.Value);
			}
			if (isBadUP.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("isBadUP", isBadUP.Value);
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("WebAuthn.setResponseOverrideBits", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> RemoveVirtualAuthenticatorAsync(string authenticatorId)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("authenticatorId", authenticatorId);
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("WebAuthn.removeVirtualAuthenticator", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> AddCredentialAsync(string authenticatorId, Credential credential)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("authenticatorId", authenticatorId);
			dictionary.Add("credential", credential.ToDictionary());
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("WebAuthn.addCredential", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<GetCredentialResponse> GetCredentialAsync(string authenticatorId, byte[] credentialId)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("authenticatorId", authenticatorId);
			dictionary.Add("credentialId", ToBase64String(credentialId));
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<GetCredentialResponse>("WebAuthn.getCredential", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<GetCredentialsResponse> GetCredentialsAsync(string authenticatorId)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("authenticatorId", authenticatorId);
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<GetCredentialsResponse>("WebAuthn.getCredentials", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> RemoveCredentialAsync(string authenticatorId, byte[] credentialId)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("authenticatorId", authenticatorId);
			dictionary.Add("credentialId", ToBase64String(credentialId));
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("WebAuthn.removeCredential", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> ClearCredentialsAsync(string authenticatorId)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("authenticatorId", authenticatorId);
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("WebAuthn.clearCredentials", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> SetUserVerifiedAsync(string authenticatorId, bool isUserVerified)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("authenticatorId", authenticatorId);
			dictionary.Add("isUserVerified", isUserVerified);
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("WebAuthn.setUserVerified", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> SetAutomaticPresenceSimulationAsync(string authenticatorId, bool enabled)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("authenticatorId", authenticatorId);
			dictionary.Add("enabled", enabled);
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("WebAuthn.setAutomaticPresenceSimulation", dictionary);
		}
	}
}

		{
			Dictionary<string, object> parameters = null;
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("WebAudio.enable", parameters);
		}

		{
			Dictionary<string, object> parameters = null;

		}
