		[DataMember]
		internal string stream { get; set; }

		public string Stream => stream;
	}
	public class FetchClient : DevToolsDomainBase
	{
		private IDevToolsClient _client;

		public event EventHandler<RequestPausedEventArgs> RequestPaused
		{
			add
			{
				_client.AddEventHandler("Fetch.requestPaused", value);
			}
			remove
			{
				_client.RemoveEventHandler("Fetch.requestPaused", value);
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler<AuthRequiredEventArgs> AuthRequired
		{
			add
			{
				_client.AddEventHandler("Fetch.authRequired", value);
			}
			remove
			{
				_client.RemoveEventHandler("Fetch.authRequired", value);
			}
		}

		public FetchClient(IDevToolsClient client)
		{
			_client = client;
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> DisableAsync()
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> parameters = null;
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("Fetch.disable", parameters);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> EnableAsync(IList<RequestPattern> patterns = null, bool? handleAuthRequests = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			if (patterns != null)
			{
				dictionary.Add("patterns", patterns.Select((RequestPattern x) => x.ToDictionary()));
			}
			if (handleAuthRequests.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("handleAuthRequests", handleAuthRequests.Value);
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("Fetch.enable", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> FailRequestAsync(string requestId, ErrorReason errorReason)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("requestId", requestId);
			dictionary.Add("errorReason", EnumToString(errorReason));
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("Fetch.failRequest", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> FulfillRequestAsync(string requestId, int responseCode, IList<HeaderEntry> responseHeaders = null, byte[] binaryResponseHeaders = null, byte[] body = null, string responsePhrase = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("requestId", requestId);
			dictionary.Add("responseCode", responseCode);
			if (responseHeaders != null)
			{
				dictionary.Add("responseHeaders", responseHeaders.Select((HeaderEntry x) => x.ToDictionary()));
			}
			if (binaryResponseHeaders != null)
			{
				dictionary.Add("binaryResponseHeaders", ToBase64String(binaryResponseHeaders));
			}
			if (body != null)
			{
				dictionary.Add("body", ToBase64String(body));
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(responsePhrase))
			{
				dictionary.Add("responsePhrase", responsePhrase);
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("Fetch.fulfillRequest", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> ContinueRequestAsync(string requestId, string url = null, string method = null, byte[] postData = null, IList<HeaderEntry> headers = null, bool? interceptResponse = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("requestId", requestId);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(url))
			{
				dictionary.Add("url", url);
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(method))
			{
				dictionary.Add("method", method);
			}
			if (postData != null)
			{
				dictionary.Add("postData", ToBase64String(postData));
			}
			if (headers != null)
			{
				dictionary.Add("headers", headers.Select((HeaderEntry x) => x.ToDictionary()));
			}
			if (interceptResponse.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("interceptResponse", interceptResponse.Value);
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("Fetch.continueRequest", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> ContinueWithAuthAsync(string requestId, AuthChallengeResponse authChallengeResponse)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("requestId", requestId);
			dictionary.Add("authChallengeResponse", authChallengeResponse.ToDictionary());
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("Fetch.continueWithAuth", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> ContinueResponseAsync(string requestId, int? responseCode = null, string responsePhrase = null, IList<HeaderEntry> responseHeaders = null, byte[] binaryResponseHeaders = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("requestId", requestId);
			if (responseCode.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("responseCode", responseCode.Value);
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(responsePhrase))
			{
				dictionary.Add("responsePhrase", responsePhrase);
			}
			if (responseHeaders != null)
			{
				dictionary.Add("responseHeaders", responseHeaders.Select((HeaderEntry x) => x.ToDictionary()));
			}
			if (binaryResponseHeaders != null)
			{
				dictionary.Add("binaryResponseHeaders", ToBase64String(binaryResponseHeaders));
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("Fetch.continueResponse", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<GetResponseBodyResponse> GetResponseBodyAsync(string requestId)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("requestId", requestId);
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<GetResponseBodyResponse>("Fetch.getResponseBody", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<TakeResponseBodyAsStreamResponse> TakeResponseBodyAsStreamAsync(string requestId)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("requestId", requestId);
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<TakeResponseBodyAsStreamResponse>("Fetch.takeResponseBodyAsStream", dictionary);
		}
	}

		public Task<RequestMemoryDumpResponse> RequestMemoryDumpAsync(bool? deterministic = null, MemoryDumpLevelOfDetail? levelOfDetail = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			if (deterministic.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("deterministic", deterministic.Value);
			}
			if (levelOfDetail.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("levelOfDetail", EnumToString(levelOfDetail));
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<RequestMemoryDumpResponse>("Tracing.requestMemoryDump", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> StartAsync(string categories = null, string options = null, double? bufferUsageReportingInterval = null, StartTransferMode? transferMode = null, StreamFormat? streamFormat = null, StreamCompression? streamCompression = null, TraceConfig traceConfig = null, byte[] perfettoConfig = null, TracingBackend? tracingBackend = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(categories))
			{
				dictionary.Add("categories", categories);
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(options))
			{
				dictionary.Add("options", options);
			}
			if (bufferUsageReportingInterval.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("bufferUsageReportingInterval", bufferUsageReportingInterval.Value);
			}
			if (transferMode.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("transferMode", EnumToString(transferMode));
			}
			if (streamFormat.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("streamFormat", EnumToString(streamFormat));
			}
			if (streamCompression.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("streamCompression", EnumToString(streamCompression));
			}
			if (traceConfig != null)
			{
				dictionary.Add("traceConfig", traceConfig.ToDictionary());
			}
			if (perfettoConfig != null)
			{
				dictionary.Add("perfettoConfig", ToBase64String(perfettoConfig));
			}
			if (tracingBackend.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("tracingBackend", EnumToString(tracingBackend));
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("Tracing.start", dictionary);
		}
	}

	}

		public Task<CloseTargetResponse> CloseTargetAsync(string targetId)

			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("targetId", targetId);
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<CloseTargetResponse>("Target.closeTarget", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> ExposeDevToolsProtocolAsync(string targetId, string bindingName = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("targetId", targetId);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(bindingName))
			{
				dictionary.Add("bindingName", bindingName);
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("Target.exposeDevToolsProtocol", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<CreateBrowserContextResponse> CreateBrowserContextAsync(bool? disposeOnDetach = null, string proxyServer = null, string proxyBypassList = null, string[] originsWithUniversalNetworkAccess = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			if (disposeOnDetach.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("disposeOnDetach", disposeOnDetach.Value);
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(proxyServer))
			{
				dictionary.Add("proxyServer", proxyServer);
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(proxyBypassList))
			{
				dictionary.Add("proxyBypassList", proxyBypassList);
			}
			if (originsWithUniversalNetworkAccess != null)
			{
				dictionary.Add("originsWithUniversalNetworkAccess", originsWithUniversalNetworkAccess);
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<CreateBrowserContextResponse>("Target.createBrowserContext", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<GetBrowserContextsResponse> GetBrowserContextsAsync()
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> parameters = null;
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<GetBrowserContextsResponse>("Target.getBrowserContexts", parameters);
		}

		public Task<CreateTargetResponse> CreateTargetAsync(string url, int? width = null, int? height = null, string browserContextId = null, bool? enableBeginFrameControl = null, bool? newWindow = null, bool? background = null, bool? forTab = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("url", url);
			if (width.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("width", width.Value);
			}
			if (height.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("height", height.Value);
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(browserContextId))
			{
				dictionary.Add("browserContextId", browserContextId);
			}
			if (enableBeginFrameControl.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("enableBeginFrameControl", enableBeginFrameControl.Value);
			}
			if (newWindow.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("newWindow", newWindow.Value);
			}
			if (background.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("background", background.Value);
			}
			if (forTab.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("forTab", forTab.Value);
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<CreateTargetResponse>("Target.createTarget", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> DetachFromTargetAsync(string sessionId = null, string targetId = null)

			{
				dictionary.Add("sessionId", sessionId);

		}

		}

		}

			get

				return (SharedStorageAccessType)DevToolsDomainEventArgsBase.StringToEnum(typeof(SharedStorageAccessType), type);
			}
			set
			{
				type = DevToolsDomainEventArgsBase.EnumToString(value);
			}
		}

		[DataMember(Name = "type", IsRequired = true)]
		internal string type { get; private set; }

		public string MainFrameId { get; private set; }

		[DataMember(Name = "ownerOrigin", IsRequired = true)]
		public string OwnerOrigin { get; private set; }

		[DataMember(Name = "params", IsRequired = true)]
		public SharedStorageAccessParams Params { get; private set; }

		public Task<GetCookiesResponse> GetCookiesAsync(string browserContextId = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(browserContextId))
			{
				dictionary.Add("browserContextId", browserContextId);
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<GetCookiesResponse>("Storage.getCookies", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> SetCookiesAsync(IList<CookieParam> cookies, string browserContextId = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("cookies", cookies.Select((CookieParam x) => x.ToDictionary()));
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(browserContextId))
			{
				dictionary.Add("browserContextId", browserContextId);
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("Storage.setCookies", dictionary);
		}

namespace CefSharp.DevTools.Security
{
	public enum MixedContentType
	{
		[EnumMember(Value = "blockable")]
		Blockable,
		[EnumMember(Value = "optionally-blockable")]
		OptionallyBlockable,
		[EnumMember(Value = "none")]
		None
	}
	public enum SecurityState
	{
		[EnumMember(Value = "unknown")]
		Unknown,
		[EnumMember(Value = "neutral")]
		Neutral,
		[EnumMember(Value = "insecure")]
		Insecure,

			}

		}

	public class PageClient : DevToolsDomainBase
	{
		private IDevToolsClient _client;

		public event EventHandler<DomContentEventFiredEventArgs> DomContentEventFired
		{
			add
			{
				_client.AddEventHandler("Page.domContentEventFired", value);
			}
			remove
			{
				_client.RemoveEventHandler("Page.domContentEventFired", value);
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler<FileChooserOpenedEventArgs> FileChooserOpened
		{
			add
			{
				_client.AddEventHandler("Page.fileChooserOpened", value);
			}
			remove
			{
				_client.RemoveEventHandler("Page.fileChooserOpened", value);
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler<FrameAttachedEventArgs> FrameAttached
		{
			add
			{
				_client.AddEventHandler("Page.frameAttached", value);
			}
			remove
			{
				_client.RemoveEventHandler("Page.frameAttached", value);
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler<FrameDetachedEventArgs> FrameDetached
		{
			add
			{
				_client.AddEventHandler("Page.frameDetached", value);
			}
			remove

		public Task<AddScriptToEvaluateOnNewDocumentResponse> AddScriptToEvaluateOnNewDocumentAsync(string source, string worldName = null, bool? includeCommandLineAPI = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("source", source);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(worldName))
			{
				dictionary.Add("worldName", worldName);
			}
			if (includeCommandLineAPI.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("includeCommandLineAPI", includeCommandLineAPI.Value);
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<AddScriptToEvaluateOnNewDocumentResponse>("Page.addScriptToEvaluateOnNewDocument", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<CaptureScreenshotResponse> CaptureScreenshotAsync(CaptureScreenshotFormat? format = null, int? quality = null, Viewport clip = null, bool? fromSurface = null, bool? captureBeyondViewport = null, bool? optimizeForSpeed = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			if (format.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("format", EnumToString(format));
			}
			if (quality.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("quality", quality.Value);
			}
			if (clip != null)
			{
				dictionary.Add("clip", clip.ToDictionary());
			}
			if (fromSurface.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("fromSurface", fromSurface.Value);
			}
			if (captureBeyondViewport.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("captureBeyondViewport", captureBeyondViewport.Value);
			}
			if (optimizeForSpeed.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("optimizeForSpeed", optimizeForSpeed.Value);
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<CaptureScreenshotResponse>("Page.captureScreenshot", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<PrintToPDFResponse> PrintToPDFAsync(bool? landscape = null, bool? displayHeaderFooter = null, bool? printBackground = null, double? scale = null, double? paperWidth = null, double? paperHeight = null, double? marginTop = null, double? marginBottom = null, double? marginLeft = null, double? marginRight = null, string pageRanges = null, string headerTemplate = null, string footerTemplate = null, bool? preferCSSPageSize = null, PrintToPDFTransferMode? transferMode = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			if (landscape.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("landscape", landscape.Value);
			}
			if (displayHeaderFooter.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("displayHeaderFooter", displayHeaderFooter.Value);
			}
			if (printBackground.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("printBackground", printBackground.Value);
			}
			if (scale.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("scale", scale.Value);
			}
			if (paperWidth.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("paperWidth", paperWidth.Value);
			}
			if (paperHeight.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("paperHeight", paperHeight.Value);
			}
			if (marginTop.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("marginTop", marginTop.Value);
			}
			if (marginBottom.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("marginBottom", marginBottom.Value);
			}
			if (marginLeft.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("marginLeft", marginLeft.Value);
			}
			if (marginRight.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("marginRight", marginRight.Value);
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(pageRanges))
			{
				dictionary.Add("pageRanges", pageRanges);
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(headerTemplate))
			{
				dictionary.Add("headerTemplate", headerTemplate);
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(footerTemplate))
			{
				dictionary.Add("footerTemplate", footerTemplate);
			}
			if (preferCSSPageSize.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("preferCSSPageSize", preferCSSPageSize.Value);
			}
			if (transferMode.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("transferMode", EnumToString(transferMode));
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<PrintToPDFResponse>("Page.printToPDF", dictionary);
		}

namespace CefSharp.DevTools.Overlay
{
	[DataContract]
	public class SourceOrderConfig : DevToolsDomainEntityBase
	{
		[DataMember(Name = "parentOutlineColor", IsRequired = true)]
		public RGBA ParentOutlineColor { get; set; }

		[DataMember(Name = "childOutlineColor", IsRequired = true)]
		public RGBA ChildOutlineColor { get; set; }
	}
	[DataContract]
	public class GridHighlightConfig : DevToolsDomainEntityBase
	{
		[DataMember(Name = "showGridExtensionLines", IsRequired = false)]
		public bool? ShowGridExtensionLines { get; set; }

		[DataMember(Name = "showPositiveLineNumbers", IsRequired = false)]
		public bool? ShowPositiveLineNumbers { get; set; }

		[DataMember(Name = "showNegativeLineNumbers", IsRequired = false)]
		public bool? ShowNegativeLineNumbers { get; set; }

		[DataMember(Name = "showAreaNames", IsRequired = false)]
		public bool? ShowAreaNames { get; set; }

		[DataMember(Name = "showLineNames", IsRequired = false)]

	public class OverlayClient : DevToolsDomainBase
	{
		private IDevToolsClient _client;

		public event EventHandler<InspectNodeRequestedEventArgs> InspectNodeRequested
		{
			add
			{
				_client.AddEventHandler("Overlay.inspectNodeRequested", value);
			}
			remove
			{
				_client.RemoveEventHandler("Overlay.inspectNodeRequested", value);
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler<NodeHighlightRequestedEventArgs> NodeHighlightRequested
		{
			add
			{
				_client.AddEventHandler("Overlay.nodeHighlightRequested", value);
			}
			remove
			{
				_client.RemoveEventHandler("Overlay.nodeHighlightRequested", value);
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler<ScreenshotRequestedEventArgs> ScreenshotRequested
		{
			add
			{
				_client.AddEventHandler("Overlay.screenshotRequested", value);
			}
			remove
			{
				_client.RemoveEventHandler("Overlay.screenshotRequested", value);
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler<EventArgs> InspectModeCanceled
		{
			add
			{
				_client.AddEventHandler("Overlay.inspectModeCanceled", value);
			}
			remove
			{
				_client.RemoveEventHandler("Overlay.inspectModeCanceled", value);
			}
		}

		public OverlayClient(IDevToolsClient client)
		{
			_client = client;
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> DisableAsync()
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> parameters = null;
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("Overlay.disable", parameters);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> EnableAsync()
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> parameters = null;
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("Overlay.enable", parameters);
		}

		public Task<GetHighlightObjectForTestResponse> GetHighlightObjectForTestAsync(int nodeId, bool? includeDistance = null, bool? includeStyle = null, ColorFormat? colorFormat = null, bool? showAccessibilityInfo = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("nodeId", nodeId);
			if (includeDistance.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("includeDistance", includeDistance.Value);
			}
			if (includeStyle.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("includeStyle", includeStyle.Value);
			}
			if (colorFormat.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("colorFormat", EnumToString(colorFormat));
			}
			if (showAccessibilityInfo.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("showAccessibilityInfo", showAccessibilityInfo.Value);
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<GetHighlightObjectForTestResponse>("Overlay.getHighlightObjectForTest", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<GetGridHighlightObjectsForTestResponse> GetGridHighlightObjectsForTestAsync(int[] nodeIds)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("nodeIds", nodeIds);
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<GetGridHighlightObjectsForTestResponse>("Overlay.getGridHighlightObjectsForTest", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<GetSourceOrderHighlightObjectForTestResponse> GetSourceOrderHighlightObjectForTestAsync(int nodeId)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("nodeId", nodeId);
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<GetSourceOrderHighlightObjectForTestResponse>("Overlay.getSourceOrderHighlightObjectForTest", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> HideHighlightAsync()
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> parameters = null;
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("Overlay.hideHighlight", parameters);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> HighlightNodeAsync(HighlightConfig highlightConfig, int? nodeId = null, int? backendNodeId = null, string objectId = null, string selector = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("highlightConfig", highlightConfig.ToDictionary());
			if (nodeId.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("nodeId", nodeId.Value);
			}
			if (backendNodeId.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("backendNodeId", backendNodeId.Value);
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(objectId))
			{
				dictionary.Add("objectId", objectId);
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(selector))
			{
				dictionary.Add("selector", selector);
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("Overlay.highlightNode", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> HighlightQuadAsync(double[] quad, RGBA color = null, RGBA outlineColor = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("quad", quad);
			if (color != null)
			{
				dictionary.Add("color", color.ToDictionary());
			}
			if (outlineColor != null)
			{
				dictionary.Add("outlineColor", outlineColor.ToDictionary());
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("Overlay.highlightQuad", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> HighlightRectAsync(int x, int y, int width, int height, RGBA color = null, RGBA outlineColor = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("x", x);
			dictionary.Add("y", y);
			dictionary.Add("width", width);
			dictionary.Add("height", height);
			if (color != null)
			{
				dictionary.Add("color", color.ToDictionary());
			}
			if (outlineColor != null)
			{
				dictionary.Add("outlineColor", outlineColor.ToDictionary());
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("Overlay.highlightRect", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> HighlightSourceOrderAsync(SourceOrderConfig sourceOrderConfig, int? nodeId = null, int? backendNodeId = null, string objectId = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("sourceOrderConfig", sourceOrderConfig.ToDictionary());
			if (nodeId.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("nodeId", nodeId.Value);
			}
			if (backendNodeId.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("backendNodeId", backendNodeId.Value);
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(objectId))
			{
				dictionary.Add("objectId", objectId);
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("Overlay.highlightSourceOrder", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> SetInspectModeAsync(InspectMode mode, HighlightConfig highlightConfig = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("mode", EnumToString(mode));
			if (highlightConfig != null)
			{
				dictionary.Add("highlightConfig", highlightConfig.ToDictionary());
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("Overlay.setInspectMode", dictionary);
		}

namespace CefSharp.DevTools.Network
{
	public enum ResourceType
	{
		[EnumMember(Value = "Document")]
		Document,
		[EnumMember(Value = "Stylesheet")]
		Stylesheet,
		[EnumMember(Value = "Image")]
		Image,
		[EnumMember(Value = "Media")]
		Media,
		[EnumMember(Value = "Font")]

	public class NetworkClient : DevToolsDomainBase
	{
		private IDevToolsClient _client;

		public event EventHandler<DataReceivedEventArgs> DataReceived
		{
			add
			{
				_client.AddEventHandler("Network.dataReceived", value);
			}
			remove
			{
				_client.RemoveEventHandler("Network.dataReceived", value);
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler<EventSourceMessageReceivedEventArgs> EventSourceMessageReceived
		{
			add
			{
				_client.AddEventHandler("Network.eventSourceMessageReceived", value);
			}
			remove
			{
				_client.RemoveEventHandler("Network.eventSourceMessageReceived", value);
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler<LoadingFailedEventArgs> LoadingFailed
		{
			add

			}
			remove

			}
		}

		{

				_client.AddEventHandler("Network.loadingFinished", value);
			}
			remove

				_client.RemoveEventHandler("Network.loadingFinished", value);
			}
		}

			}
			remove

		public Task<SetCookieResponse> SetCookieAsync(string name, string value, string url = null, string domain = null, string path = null, bool? secure = null, bool? httpOnly = null, CookieSameSite? sameSite = null, double? expires = null, CookiePriority? priority = null, bool? sameParty = null, CookieSourceScheme? sourceScheme = null, int? sourcePort = null, string partitionKey = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("name", name);
			dictionary.Add("value", value);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(url))
			{
				dictionary.Add("url", url);
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(domain))
			{
				dictionary.Add("domain", domain);
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(path))
			{
				dictionary.Add("path", path);
			}
			if (secure.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("secure", secure.Value);
			}
			if (httpOnly.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("httpOnly", httpOnly.Value);
			}
			if (sameSite.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("sameSite", EnumToString(sameSite));
			}
			if (expires.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("expires", expires.Value);
			}
			if (priority.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("priority", EnumToString(priority));
			}
			if (sameParty.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("sameParty", sameParty.Value);
			}
			if (sourceScheme.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("sourceScheme", EnumToString(sourceScheme));
			}
			if (sourcePort.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("sourcePort", sourcePort.Value);
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(partitionKey))
			{
				dictionary.Add("partitionKey", partitionKey);
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<SetCookieResponse>("Network.setCookie", dictionary);
		}

	public class Headers : Dictionary<string, string>
	{
		public Headers()
			: base((IEqualityComparer<string>)StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
		{
		}

		public Dictionary<string, string> ToDictionary()
		{
			return this;
		}

		public bool TryGetValues(string key, out string[] values)
		{
			values = null;
			if (TryGetValue(key, out var value))
			{
				List<string> list = new List<string>();
				int num = -1;
				int num2 = -1;
				bool flag = false;
				for (int i = 0; i < value.Length; i++)
				{
					char c = value[i];
					if (c == '"')
					{
						flag = !flag;
					}
					else
					{
						if (!flag && char.IsWhiteSpace(c))
						{
							continue;
						}
						if (num == -1)
						{
							num = i;
						}
						if (!flag && c == ',')
						{
							if (num2 == -1)
							{
								list.Add(string.Empty);
							}
							else
							{
								list.Add(value.Substring(num, num2 + 1 - num));
							}
							num = -1;
							num2 = -1;
						}
						else
						{
							num2 = i;
						}
					}
				}
				if (num2 == -1)
				{
					list.Add(string.Empty);
				}
				else
				{
					list.Add(value.Substring(num, num2 + 1 - num));
				}
				values = list.ToArray();
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public string[] GetCommaSeparatedValues(string key)
		{
			if (TryGetValues(key, out var result))
			{
				return result;
			}
			return null;
		}

		public void AppendCommaSeparatedValues(string key, params string[] values)
		{
			if (TryGetValue(key, out var value))
			{
				base[key] = value + "," + string.Join(",", values.Select(QuoteIfNeeded));
			}
			else
			{
				SetCommaSeparatedValues(key, values);
			}
		}

		public void SetCommaSeparatedValues(string key, params string[] values)
		{
			base[key] = string.Join(",", values.Select(QuoteIfNeeded));
		}

		private static string QuoteIfNeeded(string value)
		{
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(value) && value.Contains(',') && (value[0] != '"' || value[value.Length - 1] != '"'))
			{
				return "\"" + value + "\"";
			}
			return value;
		}
	}
}

	public enum PressureLevel
	{
		[EnumMember(Value = "moderate")]
		Moderate,
		[EnumMember(Value = "critical")]

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> StartSamplingAsync(int? samplingInterval = null, bool? suppressRandomness = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			if (samplingInterval.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("samplingInterval", samplingInterval.Value);
			}
			if (suppressRandomness.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("suppressRandomness", suppressRandomness.Value);
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("Memory.startSampling", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> StopSamplingAsync()
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> parameters = null;
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("Memory.stopSampling", parameters);
		}

		public Task<GetAllTimeSamplingProfileResponse> GetAllTimeSamplingProfileAsync()
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> parameters = null;
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<GetAllTimeSamplingProfileResponse>("Memory.getAllTimeSamplingProfile", parameters);
		}

		public Task<GetBrowserSamplingProfileResponse> GetBrowserSamplingProfileAsync()
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> parameters = null;
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<GetBrowserSamplingProfileResponse>("Memory.getBrowserSamplingProfile", parameters);
		}

		public Task<GetSamplingProfileResponse> GetSamplingProfileAsync()
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> parameters = null;
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<GetSamplingProfileResponse>("Memory.getSamplingProfile", parameters);
		}
	}

			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("LayerTree.enable", parameters);

			dictionary.Add("tiles", tiles.Select((PictureTile x) => x.ToDictionary()));
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<LoadSnapshotResponse>("LayerTree.loadSnapshot", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<MakeSnapshotResponse> MakeSnapshotAsync(string layerId)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("layerId", layerId);
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<MakeSnapshotResponse>("LayerTree.makeSnapshot", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<ProfileSnapshotResponse> ProfileSnapshotAsync(string snapshotId, int? minRepeatCount = null, double? minDuration = null, CefSharp.DevTools.DOM.Rect clipRect = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("snapshotId", snapshotId);
			if (minRepeatCount.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("minRepeatCount", minRepeatCount.Value);
			}
			if (minDuration.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("minDuration", minDuration.Value);
			}
			if (clipRect != null)
			{
				dictionary.Add("clipRect", clipRect.ToDictionary());
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<ProfileSnapshotResponse>("LayerTree.profileSnapshot", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> ReleaseSnapshotAsync(string snapshotId)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("snapshotId", snapshotId);
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("LayerTree.releaseSnapshot", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<ReplaySnapshotResponse> ReplaySnapshotAsync(string snapshotId, int? fromStep = null, int? toStep = null, double? scale = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("snapshotId", snapshotId);
			if (fromStep.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("fromStep", fromStep.Value);
			}
			if (toStep.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("toStep", toStep.Value);
			}
			if (scale.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("scale", scale.Value);
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<ReplaySnapshotResponse>("LayerTree.replaySnapshot", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<SnapshotCommandLogResponse> SnapshotCommandLogAsync(string snapshotId)

			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("snapshotId", snapshotId);
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<SnapshotCommandLogResponse>("LayerTree.snapshotCommandLog", dictionary);
		}
	}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> DispatchDragEventAsync(DispatchDragEventType type, double x, double y, DragData data, int? modifiers = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("type", EnumToString(type));
			dictionary.Add("x", x);
			dictionary.Add("y", y);
			dictionary.Add("data", data.ToDictionary());
			if (modifiers.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("modifiers", modifiers.Value);
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("Input.dispatchDragEvent", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> DispatchKeyEventAsync(DispatchKeyEventType type, int? modifiers = null, double? timestamp = null, string text = null, string unmodifiedText = null, string keyIdentifier = null, string code = null, string key = null, int? windowsVirtualKeyCode = null, int? nativeVirtualKeyCode = null, bool? autoRepeat = null, bool? isKeypad = null, bool? isSystemKey = null, int? location = null, string[] commands = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("type", EnumToString(type));
			if (modifiers.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("modifiers", modifiers.Value);
			}
			if (timestamp.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("timestamp", timestamp.Value);
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
			{
				dictionary.Add("text", text);
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(unmodifiedText))
			{
				dictionary.Add("unmodifiedText", unmodifiedText);
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(keyIdentifier))
			{
				dictionary.Add("keyIdentifier", keyIdentifier);
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(code))
			{
				dictionary.Add("code", code);
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(key))
			{
				dictionary.Add("key", key);
			}
			if (windowsVirtualKeyCode.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("windowsVirtualKeyCode", windowsVirtualKeyCode.Value);
			}
			if (nativeVirtualKeyCode.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("nativeVirtualKeyCode", nativeVirtualKeyCode.Value);
			}
			if (autoRepeat.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("autoRepeat", autoRepeat.Value);
			}
			if (isKeypad.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("isKeypad", isKeypad.Value);
			}
			if (isSystemKey.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("isSystemKey", isSystemKey.Value);
			}
			if (location.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("location", location.Value);
			}
			if (commands != null)
			{
				dictionary.Add("commands", commands);
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("Input.dispatchKeyEvent", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> DispatchMouseEventAsync(DispatchMouseEventType type, double x, double y, int? modifiers = null, double? timestamp = null, MouseButton? button = null, int? buttons = null, int? clickCount = null, double? force = null, double? tangentialPressure = null, int? tiltX = null, int? tiltY = null, int? twist = null, double? deltaX = null, double? deltaY = null, DispatchMouseEventPointerType? pointerType = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("type", EnumToString(type));
			dictionary.Add("x", x);
			dictionary.Add("y", y);
			if (modifiers.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("modifiers", modifiers.Value);
			}
			if (timestamp.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("timestamp", timestamp.Value);
			}
			if (button.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("button", EnumToString(button));
			}
			if (buttons.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("buttons", buttons.Value);
			}
			if (clickCount.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("clickCount", clickCount.Value);
			}
			if (force.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("force", force.Value);
			}
			if (tangentialPressure.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("tangentialPressure", tangentialPressure.Value);
			}
			if (tiltX.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("tiltX", tiltX.Value);
			}
			if (tiltY.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("tiltY", tiltY.Value);
			}
			if (twist.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("twist", twist.Value);
			}
			if (deltaX.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("deltaX", deltaX.Value);
			}
			if (deltaY.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("deltaY", deltaY.Value);
			}
			if (pointerType.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("pointerType", EnumToString(pointerType));
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("Input.dispatchMouseEvent", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> DispatchTouchEventAsync(DispatchTouchEventType type, IList<TouchPoint> touchPoints, int? modifiers = null, double? timestamp = null)

		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> ClearObjectStoreAsync(string databaseName, string objectStoreName, string securityOrigin = null, string storageKey = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("databaseName", databaseName);
			dictionary.Add("objectStoreName", objectStoreName);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(securityOrigin))
			{
				dictionary.Add("securityOrigin", securityOrigin);
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(storageKey))
			{
				dictionary.Add("storageKey", storageKey);
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("IndexedDB.clearObjectStore", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> DeleteDatabaseAsync(string databaseName, string securityOrigin = null, string storageKey = null)
		{

	{

		internal bool hasDamage { get; set; }

	{

		{
			_client = client;
		}

		public Task<BeginFrameResponse> BeginFrameAsync(double? frameTimeTicks = null, double? interval = null, bool? noDisplayUpdates = null, ScreenshotParams screenshot = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			if (frameTimeTicks.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("frameTimeTicks", frameTimeTicks.Value);
			}
			if (interval.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("interval", interval.Value);
			}
			if (noDisplayUpdates.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("noDisplayUpdates", noDisplayUpdates.Value);
			}
			if (screenshot != null)
			{
				dictionary.Add("screenshot", screenshot.ToDictionary());
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<BeginFrameResponse>("HeadlessExperimental.beginFrame", dictionary);
		}
	}

	public class ScreenOrientation : DevToolsDomainEntityBase

		public ScreenOrientationType Type
		{
			get

				return (ScreenOrientationType)DevToolsDomainEntityBase.StringToEnum(typeof(ScreenOrientationType), type);
			}

			{
				type = DevToolsDomainEntityBase.EnumToString(value);
			}
		}

		[DataMember(Name = "type", IsRequired = true)]

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> SetDeviceMetricsOverrideAsync(int width, int height, double deviceScaleFactor, bool mobile, double? scale = null, int? screenWidth = null, int? screenHeight = null, int? positionX = null, int? positionY = null, bool? dontSetVisibleSize = null, ScreenOrientation screenOrientation = null, Viewport viewport = null, DisplayFeature displayFeature = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("width", width);
			dictionary.Add("height", height);
			dictionary.Add("deviceScaleFactor", deviceScaleFactor);
			dictionary.Add("mobile", mobile);
			if (scale.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("scale", scale.Value);
			}
			if (screenWidth.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("screenWidth", screenWidth.Value);
			}
			if (screenHeight.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("screenHeight", screenHeight.Value);
			}
			if (positionX.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("positionX", positionX.Value);
			}
			if (positionY.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("positionY", positionY.Value);
			}
			if (dontSetVisibleSize.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("dontSetVisibleSize", dontSetVisibleSize.Value);
			}
			if (screenOrientation != null)
			{
				dictionary.Add("screenOrientation", screenOrientation.ToDictionary());
			}
			if (viewport != null)
			{
				dictionary.Add("viewport", viewport.ToDictionary());
			}
			if (displayFeature != null)
			{
				dictionary.Add("displayFeature", displayFeature.ToDictionary());
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("Emulation.setDeviceMetricsOverride", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> SetScrollbarsHiddenAsync(bool hidden)
		{

		}

	public class CaptureSnapshotResponse : DevToolsDomainResponseBase
	{
		[DataMember]
		internal IList<DocumentSnapshot> documents { get; set; }

	public class DOMSnapshotClient : DevToolsDomainBase
	{
		private IDevToolsClient _client;

		public DOMSnapshotClient(IDevToolsClient client)
		{
			_client = client;
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> DisableAsync()
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> parameters = null;
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("DOMSnapshot.disable", parameters);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> EnableAsync()
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> parameters = null;
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("DOMSnapshot.enable", parameters);
		}

		public Task<CaptureSnapshotResponse> CaptureSnapshotAsync(string[] computedStyles, bool? includePaintOrder = null, bool? includeDOMRects = null, bool? includeBlendedBackgroundColors = null, bool? includeTextColorOpacities = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("computedStyles", computedStyles);
			if (includePaintOrder.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("includePaintOrder", includePaintOrder.Value);
			}
			if (includeDOMRects.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("includeDOMRects", includeDOMRects.Value);
			}
			if (includeBlendedBackgroundColors.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("includeBlendedBackgroundColors", includeBlendedBackgroundColors.Value);
			}
			if (includeTextColorOpacities.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("includeTextColorOpacities", includeTextColorOpacities.Value);
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<CaptureSnapshotResponse>("DOMSnapshot.captureSnapshot", dictionary);
		}
	}
}

	public class DOMDebuggerClient : DevToolsDomainBase
	{
		private IDevToolsClient _client;

		public DOMDebuggerClient(IDevToolsClient client)
		{
			_client = client;
		}

		public Task<GetEventListenersResponse> GetEventListenersAsync(string objectId, int? depth = null, bool? pierce = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("objectId", objectId);
			if (depth.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("depth", depth.Value);
			}
			if (pierce.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("pierce", pierce.Value);
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<GetEventListenersResponse>("DOMDebugger.getEventListeners", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> RemoveDOMBreakpointAsync(int nodeId, DOMBreakpointType type)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("nodeId", nodeId);
			dictionary.Add("type", EnumToString(type));
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("DOMDebugger.removeDOMBreakpoint", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> RemoveEventListenerBreakpointAsync(string eventName, string targetName = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("eventName", eventName);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(targetName))
			{
				dictionary.Add("targetName", targetName);
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("DOMDebugger.removeEventListenerBreakpoint", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> RemoveInstrumentationBreakpointAsync(string eventName)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("eventName", eventName);
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("DOMDebugger.removeInstrumentationBreakpoint", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> RemoveXHRBreakpointAsync(string url)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("url", url);
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("DOMDebugger.removeXHRBreakpoint", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> SetBreakOnCSPViolationAsync(CSPViolationType[] violationTypes)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("violationTypes", EnumToString(violationTypes));
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("DOMDebugger.setBreakOnCSPViolation", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> SetDOMBreakpointAsync(int nodeId, DOMBreakpointType type)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("nodeId", nodeId);
			dictionary.Add("type", EnumToString(type));
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("DOMDebugger.setDOMBreakpoint", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> SetEventListenerBreakpointAsync(string eventName, string targetName = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("eventName", eventName);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(targetName))
			{
				dictionary.Add("targetName", targetName);
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("DOMDebugger.setEventListenerBreakpoint", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> SetInstrumentationBreakpointAsync(string eventName)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("eventName", eventName);
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("DOMDebugger.setInstrumentationBreakpoint", dictionary);

		public Node ContentDocument { get; set; }

		public IList<Node> ShadowRoots { get; set; }

		[DataMember(Name = "templateContent", IsRequired = false)]
		public Node TemplateContent { get; set; }

		[DataMember(Name = "pseudoElements", IsRequired = false)]
		public IList<Node> PseudoElements { get; set; }

		[DataMember(Name = "importedDocument", IsRequired = false)]
		public Node ImportedDocument { get; set; }

		[DataMember(Name = "distributedNodes", IsRequired = false)]
		public IList<BackendNode> DistributedNodes { get; set; }

		[DataMember(Name = "isSVG", IsRequired = false)]
		public bool? IsSVG { get; set; }

		public CompatibilityMode? CompatibilityMode
		{
			get
			{
				return (CompatibilityMode?)DevToolsDomainEntityBase.StringToEnum(typeof(CompatibilityMode?), compatibilityMode);
			}
			set
			{
				compatibilityMode = DevToolsDomainEntityBase.EnumToString(value);
			}
		}

		[DataMember(Name = "compatibilityMode", IsRequired = false)]
		internal string compatibilityMode { get; set; }

		[DataMember(Name = "assignedSlot", IsRequired = false)]
		public BackendNode AssignedSlot { get; set; }
	}

	public class DOMClient : DevToolsDomainBase
	{
		private IDevToolsClient _client;

		public event EventHandler<AttributeModifiedEventArgs> AttributeModified
		{
			add
			{
				_client.AddEventHandler("DOM.attributeModified", value);
			}
			remove
			{
				_client.RemoveEventHandler("DOM.attributeModified", value);
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler<AttributeRemovedEventArgs> AttributeRemoved
		{
			add
			{
				_client.AddEventHandler("DOM.attributeRemoved", value);
			}
			remove
			{
				_client.RemoveEventHandler("DOM.attributeRemoved", value);
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler<CharacterDataModifiedEventArgs> CharacterDataModified
		{
			add
			{
				_client.AddEventHandler("DOM.characterDataModified", value);
			}
			remove
			{
				_client.RemoveEventHandler("DOM.characterDataModified", value);
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler<ChildNodeCountUpdatedEventArgs> ChildNodeCountUpdated

		public DOMClient(IDevToolsClient client)
		{
			_client = client;
		}

		public Task<CollectClassNamesFromSubtreeResponse> CollectClassNamesFromSubtreeAsync(int nodeId)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("nodeId", nodeId);
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<CollectClassNamesFromSubtreeResponse>("DOM.collectClassNamesFromSubtree", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<CopyToResponse> CopyToAsync(int nodeId, int targetNodeId, int? insertBeforeNodeId = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("nodeId", nodeId);
			dictionary.Add("targetNodeId", targetNodeId);
			if (insertBeforeNodeId.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("insertBeforeNodeId", insertBeforeNodeId.Value);
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<CopyToResponse>("DOM.copyTo", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<DescribeNodeResponse> DescribeNodeAsync(int? nodeId = null, int? backendNodeId = null, string objectId = null, int? depth = null, bool? pierce = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			if (nodeId.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("nodeId", nodeId.Value);
			}
			if (backendNodeId.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("backendNodeId", backendNodeId.Value);
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(objectId))
			{
				dictionary.Add("objectId", objectId);
			}
			if (depth.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("depth", depth.Value);
			}
			if (pierce.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("pierce", pierce.Value);
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DescribeNodeResponse>("DOM.describeNode", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> ScrollIntoViewIfNeededAsync(int? nodeId = null, int? backendNodeId = null, string objectId = null, Rect rect = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			if (nodeId.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("nodeId", nodeId.Value);
			}
			if (backendNodeId.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("backendNodeId", backendNodeId.Value);
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(objectId))
			{
				dictionary.Add("objectId", objectId);
			}
			if (rect != null)
			{
				dictionary.Add("rect", rect.ToDictionary());
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("DOM.scrollIntoViewIfNeeded", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> DisableAsync()
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> parameters = null;
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("DOM.disable", parameters);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> DiscardSearchResultsAsync(string searchId)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("searchId", searchId);
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("DOM.discardSearchResults", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> EnableAsync(EnableIncludeWhitespace? includeWhitespace = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			if (includeWhitespace.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("includeWhitespace", EnumToString(includeWhitespace));
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("DOM.enable", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<GetDocumentResponse> GetDocumentAsync(int? depth = null, bool? pierce = null)

		}

		public Task<GetNodesForSubtreeByStyleResponse> GetNodesForSubtreeByStyleAsync(int nodeId, IList<CSSComputedStyleProperty> computedStyles, bool? pierce = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("nodeId", nodeId);
			dictionary.Add("computedStyles", computedStyles.Select((CSSComputedStyleProperty x) => x.ToDictionary()));
			if (pierce.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("pierce", pierce.Value);
			}

	public class CastClient : DevToolsDomainBase
	{

		{
			add
			{
				_client.AddEventHandler("Cast.sinksUpdated", value);
			}
			remove
			{
				_client.RemoveEventHandler("Cast.sinksUpdated", value);
			}
		}

		{
			add
			{
				_client.AddEventHandler("Cast.issueUpdated", value);
			}
			remove
			{
				_client.RemoveEventHandler("Cast.issueUpdated", value);
			}
		}

namespace CefSharp.DevTools.CacheStorage

	public enum CachedResponseType
	{
		[EnumMember(Value = "basic")]
		Basic,
		[EnumMember(Value = "cors")]
		Cors,
		[EnumMember(Value = "default")]
		Default,
		[EnumMember(Value = "error")]
		Error,
		[EnumMember(Value = "opaqueResponse")]
		OpaqueResponse,
		[EnumMember(Value = "opaqueRedirect")]
		OpaqueRedirect
	}
	[DataContract]
	public class DataEntry : DevToolsDomainEntityBase
	{
		[DataMember(Name = "requestURL", IsRequired = true)]
		public string RequestURL { get; set; }

		[DataMember(Name = "requestMethod", IsRequired = true)]
		public string RequestMethod { get; set; }

		[DataMember(Name = "requestHeaders", IsRequired = true)]
		public IList<Header> RequestHeaders { get; set; }

		[DataMember(Name = "responseTime", IsRequired = true)]
		public double ResponseTime { get; set; }

		[DataMember(Name = "responseStatus", IsRequired = true)]
		public int ResponseStatus { get; set; }

		[DataMember(Name = "responseStatusText", IsRequired = true)]
		public string ResponseStatusText { get; set; }

		public CachedResponseType ResponseType
		{
			get
			{
				return (CachedResponseType)DevToolsDomainEntityBase.StringToEnum(typeof(CachedResponseType), responseType);
			}
			set
			{
				responseType = DevToolsDomainEntityBase.EnumToString(value);
			}
		}

		[DataMember(Name = "responseType", IsRequired = true)]
		internal string responseType { get; set; }

		[DataMember(Name = "responseHeaders", IsRequired = true)]
		public IList<Header> ResponseHeaders { get; set; }
	}

	[DataContract]
	public class RequestEntriesResponse : DevToolsDomainResponseBase
	{
		[DataMember]
		internal IList<DataEntry> cacheDataEntries { get; set; }

		public IList<DataEntry> CacheDataEntries => cacheDataEntries;

		[DataMember]
		internal double returnCount { get; set; }

		public double ReturnCount => returnCount;
	}
	public class CacheStorageClient : DevToolsDomainBase
	{
		private IDevToolsClient _client;

		public CacheStorageClient(IDevToolsClient client)
		{
			_client = client;
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> DeleteCacheAsync(string cacheId)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("cacheId", cacheId);
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("CacheStorage.deleteCache", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> DeleteEntryAsync(string cacheId, string request)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("cacheId", cacheId);
			dictionary.Add("request", request);
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("CacheStorage.deleteEntry", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<RequestCacheNamesResponse> RequestCacheNamesAsync(string securityOrigin = null, string storageKey = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(securityOrigin))
			{
				dictionary.Add("securityOrigin", securityOrigin);
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(storageKey))
			{
				dictionary.Add("storageKey", storageKey);
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<RequestCacheNamesResponse>("CacheStorage.requestCacheNames", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<RequestCachedResponseResponse> RequestCachedResponseAsync(string cacheId, string requestURL, IList<Header> requestHeaders)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("cacheId", cacheId);
			dictionary.Add("requestURL", requestURL);
			dictionary.Add("requestHeaders", requestHeaders.Select((Header x) => x.ToDictionary()));
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<RequestCachedResponseResponse>("CacheStorage.requestCachedResponse", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<RequestEntriesResponse> RequestEntriesAsync(string cacheId, int? skipCount = null, int? pageSize = null, string pathFilter = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("cacheId", cacheId);
			if (skipCount.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("skipCount", skipCount.Value);
			}
			if (pageSize.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("pageSize", pageSize.Value);
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(pathFilter))
			{
				dictionary.Add("pathFilter", pathFilter);
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<RequestEntriesResponse>("CacheStorage.requestEntries", dictionary);
		}
	}

	public class CSSClient : DevToolsDomainBase
	{
		private IDevToolsClient _client;

		public event EventHandler<FontsUpdatedEventArgs> FontsUpdated
		{
			add
			{
				_client.AddEventHandler("CSS.fontsUpdated", value);
			}
			remove
			{
				_client.RemoveEventHandler("CSS.fontsUpdated", value);
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler<EventArgs> MediaQueryResultChanged
		{
			add
			{
				_client.AddEventHandler("CSS.mediaQueryResultChanged", value);
			}
			remove
			{
				_client.RemoveEventHandler("CSS.mediaQueryResultChanged", value);
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler<StyleSheetAddedEventArgs> StyleSheetAdded
		{
			add
			{
				_client.AddEventHandler("CSS.styleSheetAdded", value);
			}
			remove
			{
				_client.RemoveEventHandler("CSS.styleSheetAdded", value);
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler<StyleSheetChangedEventArgs> StyleSheetChanged
		{
			add
			{
				_client.AddEventHandler("CSS.styleSheetChanged", value);
			}
			remove
			{
				_client.RemoveEventHandler("CSS.styleSheetChanged", value);
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler<StyleSheetRemovedEventArgs> StyleSheetRemoved
		{
			add
			{
				_client.AddEventHandler("CSS.styleSheetRemoved", value);
			}
			remove
			{
				_client.RemoveEventHandler("CSS.styleSheetRemoved", value);
			}
		}

		public CSSClient(IDevToolsClient client)
		{
			_client = client;
		}

		public Task<AddRuleResponse> AddRuleAsync(string styleSheetId, string ruleText, SourceRange location)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("styleSheetId", styleSheetId);
			dictionary.Add("ruleText", ruleText);
			dictionary.Add("location", location.ToDictionary());
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<AddRuleResponse>("CSS.addRule", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<CollectClassNamesResponse> CollectClassNamesAsync(string styleSheetId)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("styleSheetId", styleSheetId);
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<CollectClassNamesResponse>("CSS.collectClassNames", dictionary);
		}

	public enum WindowState

	[DataContract]
	public class Bounds : DevToolsDomainEntityBase
	{
		[DataMember(Name = "left", IsRequired = false)]
		public int? Left { get; set; }

		[DataMember(Name = "top", IsRequired = false)]
		public int? Top { get; set; }

		[DataMember(Name = "width", IsRequired = false)]
		public int? Width { get; set; }

		[DataMember(Name = "height", IsRequired = false)]
		public int? Height { get; set; }

		public WindowState? WindowState
		{
			get
			{
				return (WindowState?)DevToolsDomainEntityBase.StringToEnum(typeof(WindowState?), windowState);
			}
			set
			{
				windowState = DevToolsDomainEntityBase.EnumToString(value);
			}
		}

		[DataMember(Name = "windowState", IsRequired = false)]
		internal string windowState { get; set; }
	}

	public enum SetDownloadBehaviorBehavior
	{
		[EnumMember(Value = "deny")]
		Deny,
		[EnumMember(Value = "allow")]
		Allow,
		[EnumMember(Value = "allowAndName")]
		AllowAndName,

		Default
	}
	public class BrowserClient : DevToolsDomainBase
	{
		private IDevToolsClient _client;

		public event EventHandler<DownloadWillBeginEventArgs> DownloadWillBegin
		{
			add
			{
				_client.AddEventHandler("Browser.downloadWillBegin", value);
			}
			remove
			{
				_client.RemoveEventHandler("Browser.downloadWillBegin", value);
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler<DownloadProgressEventArgs> DownloadProgress
		{
			add
			{
				_client.AddEventHandler("Browser.downloadProgress", value);
			}
			remove
			{
				_client.RemoveEventHandler("Browser.downloadProgress", value);
			}
		}

		public BrowserClient(IDevToolsClient client)
		{
			_client = client;
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> SetPermissionAsync(PermissionDescriptor permission, PermissionSetting setting, string origin = null, string browserContextId = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("permission", permission.ToDictionary());
			dictionary.Add("setting", EnumToString(setting));
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(origin))
			{
				dictionary.Add("origin", origin);
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(browserContextId))
			{
				dictionary.Add("browserContextId", browserContextId);
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("Browser.setPermission", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> GrantPermissionsAsync(PermissionType[] permissions, string origin = null, string browserContextId = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("permissions", EnumToString(permissions));
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(origin))
			{
				dictionary.Add("origin", origin);
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(browserContextId))
			{
				dictionary.Add("browserContextId", browserContextId);
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("Browser.grantPermissions", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> ResetPermissionsAsync(string browserContextId = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(browserContextId))
			{
				dictionary.Add("browserContextId", browserContextId);
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("Browser.resetPermissions", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> SetDownloadBehaviorAsync(SetDownloadBehaviorBehavior behavior, string browserContextId = null, string downloadPath = null, bool? eventsEnabled = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("behavior", EnumToString(behavior));
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(browserContextId))
			{
				dictionary.Add("browserContextId", browserContextId);
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(downloadPath))
			{
				dictionary.Add("downloadPath", downloadPath);
			}
			if (eventsEnabled.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("eventsEnabled", eventsEnabled.Value);
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("Browser.setDownloadBehavior", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> CancelDownloadAsync(string guid, string browserContextId = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("guid", guid);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(browserContextId))
			{
				dictionary.Add("browserContextId", browserContextId);
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("Browser.cancelDownload", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> CloseAsync()

			Dictionary<string, object> parameters = null;
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("Browser.close", parameters);
		}

		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> SetDockTileAsync(string badgeLabel = null, byte[] image = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(badgeLabel))
			{
				dictionary.Add("badgeLabel", badgeLabel);
			}
			if (image != null)
			{
				dictionary.Add("image", ToBase64String(image));
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("Browser.setDockTile", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> ExecuteBrowserCommandAsync(BrowserCommandId commandId)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("commandId", EnumToString(commandId));
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("Browser.executeBrowserCommand", dictionary);
		}

namespace CefSharp.DevTools.BackgroundService
{
	public enum ServiceName
	{
		[EnumMember(Value = "backgroundFetch")]
		BackgroundFetch,
		[EnumMember(Value = "backgroundSync")]
		BackgroundSync,
		[EnumMember(Value = "pushMessaging")]
		PushMessaging,
		[EnumMember(Value = "notifications")]
		Notifications,
		[EnumMember(Value = "paymentHandler")]
		PaymentHandler,
		[EnumMember(Value = "periodicBackgroundSync")]
		PeriodicBackgroundSync

		public BackgroundServiceEvent BackgroundServiceEvent { get; private set; }

	public class BackgroundServiceClient : DevToolsDomainBase
	{
		private IDevToolsClient _client;

		public event EventHandler<RecordingStateChangedEventArgs> RecordingStateChanged
		{
			add
			{
				_client.AddEventHandler("BackgroundService.recordingStateChanged", value);
			}
			remove
			{
				_client.RemoveEventHandler("BackgroundService.recordingStateChanged", value);
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler<BackgroundServiceEventReceivedEventArgs> BackgroundServiceEventReceived
		{
			add
			{
				_client.AddEventHandler("BackgroundService.backgroundServiceEventReceived", value);
			}
			remove
			{
				_client.RemoveEventHandler("BackgroundService.backgroundServiceEventReceived", value);
			}
		}

		public BackgroundServiceClient(IDevToolsClient client)
		{
			_client = client;
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> StartObservingAsync(ServiceName service)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("service", EnumToString(service));
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("BackgroundService.startObserving", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> StopObservingAsync(ServiceName service)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("service", EnumToString(service));
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("BackgroundService.stopObserving", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> SetRecordingAsync(bool shouldRecord, ServiceName service)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("shouldRecord", shouldRecord);
			dictionary.Add("service", EnumToString(service));
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("BackgroundService.setRecording", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> ClearEventsAsync(ServiceName service)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("service", EnumToString(service));
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("BackgroundService.clearEvents", dictionary);
		}
	}
}

	public class GetEncodedResponseResponse : DevToolsDomainResponseBase
	{
		[DataMember]
		internal string body { get; set; }

		public byte[] Body => Convert(body);

		[DataMember]
		internal int originalSize { get; set; }

		public int OriginalSize => originalSize;

		[DataMember]
		internal int encodedSize { get; set; }

		public int EncodedSize => encodedSize;
	}
	public enum GetEncodedResponseEncoding
	{
		[EnumMember(Value = "webp")]
		Webp,
		[EnumMember(Value = "jpeg")]
		Jpeg,
		[EnumMember(Value = "png")]
		Png
	}
	public class AuditsClient : DevToolsDomainBase
	{
		private IDevToolsClient _client;

		public event EventHandler<IssueAddedEventArgs> IssueAdded
		{
			add
			{
				_client.AddEventHandler("Audits.issueAdded", value);
			}
			remove
			{
				_client.RemoveEventHandler("Audits.issueAdded", value);
			}
		}

		public AuditsClient(IDevToolsClient client)
		{
			_client = client;
		}

		public Task<GetEncodedResponseResponse> GetEncodedResponseAsync(string requestId, GetEncodedResponseEncoding encoding, double? quality = null, bool? sizeOnly = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("requestId", requestId);
			dictionary.Add("encoding", EnumToString(encoding));
			if (quality.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("quality", quality.Value);
			}
			if (sizeOnly.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("sizeOnly", sizeOnly.Value);
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<GetEncodedResponseResponse>("Audits.getEncodedResponse", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> DisableAsync()
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> parameters = null;
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("Audits.disable", parameters);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> EnableAsync()
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> parameters = null;
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("Audits.enable", parameters);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> CheckContrastAsync(bool? reportAAA = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			if (reportAAA.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("reportAAA", reportAAA.Value);
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("Audits.checkContrast", dictionary);
		}
	}

	public class AccessibilityClient : DevToolsDomainBase
	{
		private IDevToolsClient _client;

		public event EventHandler<LoadCompleteEventArgs> LoadComplete
		{
			add
			{
				_client.AddEventHandler("Accessibility.loadComplete", value);
			}
			remove
			{
				_client.RemoveEventHandler("Accessibility.loadComplete", value);
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler<NodesUpdatedEventArgs> NodesUpdated
		{
			add
			{
				_client.AddEventHandler("Accessibility.nodesUpdated", value);
			}
			remove
			{
				_client.RemoveEventHandler("Accessibility.nodesUpdated", value);
			}
		}

		public AccessibilityClient(IDevToolsClient client)
		{
			_client = client;
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> DisableAsync()
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> parameters = null;
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("Accessibility.disable", parameters);
		}

		public Task<DevToolsMethodResponse> EnableAsync()
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> parameters = null;
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<DevToolsMethodResponse>("Accessibility.enable", parameters);
		}

		public Task<GetPartialAXTreeResponse> GetPartialAXTreeAsync(int? nodeId = null, int? backendNodeId = null, string objectId = null, bool? fetchRelatives = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			if (nodeId.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("nodeId", nodeId.Value);
			}
			if (backendNodeId.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("backendNodeId", backendNodeId.Value);
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(objectId))
			{
				dictionary.Add("objectId", objectId);
			}
			if (fetchRelatives.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("fetchRelatives", fetchRelatives.Value);
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<GetPartialAXTreeResponse>("Accessibility.getPartialAXTree", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<GetFullAXTreeResponse> GetFullAXTreeAsync(int? depth = null, string frameId = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			if (depth.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("depth", depth.Value);
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(frameId))
			{
				dictionary.Add("frameId", frameId);
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<GetFullAXTreeResponse>("Accessibility.getFullAXTree", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<GetRootAXNodeResponse> GetRootAXNodeAsync(string frameId = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(frameId))
			{
				dictionary.Add("frameId", frameId);
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<GetRootAXNodeResponse>("Accessibility.getRootAXNode", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<GetAXNodeAndAncestorsResponse> GetAXNodeAndAncestorsAsync(int? nodeId = null, int? backendNodeId = null, string objectId = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			if (nodeId.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("nodeId", nodeId.Value);
			}
			if (backendNodeId.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("backendNodeId", backendNodeId.Value);
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(objectId))
			{
				dictionary.Add("objectId", objectId);
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<GetAXNodeAndAncestorsResponse>("Accessibility.getAXNodeAndAncestors", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<GetChildAXNodesResponse> GetChildAXNodesAsync(string id, string frameId = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			dictionary.Add("id", id);
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(frameId))
			{
				dictionary.Add("frameId", frameId);
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<GetChildAXNodesResponse>("Accessibility.getChildAXNodes", dictionary);
		}

		public Task<QueryAXTreeResponse> QueryAXTreeAsync(int? nodeId = null, int? backendNodeId = null, string objectId = null, string accessibleName = null, string role = null)
		{
			Dictionary<string, object> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, object>();
			if (nodeId.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("nodeId", nodeId.Value);
			}
			if (backendNodeId.HasValue)
			{
				dictionary.Add("backendNodeId", backendNodeId.Value);
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(objectId))
			{
				dictionary.Add("objectId", objectId);
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(accessibleName))
			{
				dictionary.Add("accessibleName", accessibleName);
			}
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(role))
			{
				dictionary.Add("role", role);
			}
			return _client.ExecuteDevToolsMethodAsync<QueryAXTreeResponse>("Accessibility.queryAXTree", dictionary);
		}
	}

			Assembly assembly = Assembly.LoadFrom(assemblyFile);
			Type type = assembly.GetType("CefSharp.BrowserSubprocess.BrowserSubprocessExecutable");
			object obj = Activator.CreateInstance(type);
			MethodInfo method = type.GetMethod("MainSelfHost", BindingFlags.Static | BindingFlags.Public);
			ParameterInfo[] parameters = method.GetParameters();
			object[] parameters2 = new object[1] { args };
			object obj2 = method.Invoke(null, parameters2);
			return (int)obj2;
		}
	}
